const SHEET_IDS = {
  LOGIN: '11EX6hB9-XDZ2dEQVmYqPzLr7MFGVnmCRtVIsTxb3Z00',
  OPTIONS: '1G1xpEIcd4xyMBCqlmzd2_IaPY7B8gDv_0I4qgCrBjCw'
};

const PHOTO_FOLDER_ID = '18ZTL-DldvkI7UNSDBWQlg6DneHz9a3tf';

const SHEET_NAMES = {
  USER: 'ປະຫວັດ ອສບ',
  VILLAGE: 'village',
  HOUSEHOLD: 'ຫົວໜ້າຄອບຄົວ',
  MEMBER: 'ສະມາຊິກຄອບຄົວ',
  MOBILE: 'ປະຊາກອນເຄື່ອນທີ່',
  LAND: 'ຂໍ້ມູນທີ່ດິນ',
  VEHICLE: 'ຂໍ້ມູນຍານພະຫະນະ',
  OPTIONS: 'ຕົວເລືອກ'
};

// ===================================
// Serve HTML
// ===================================
function doGet() {
  return HtmlService.createHtmlOutputFromFile('Index')
    .setTitle('ລະບົບຂໍ້ມູນປະຊາກອນ')
    .setXFrameOptionsMode(HtmlService.XFrameOptionsMode.ALLOWALL);
}

// ===================================
// Upload Photo
// ===================================
function uploadPhoto(photoData, fileName) {
  try {
    // ຖ້າເປັນ URL ຢູ່ແລ້ວ (ຮູບທີ່ມີຢູ່) ໃຫ້ສົ່ງຄືນຄ່າກັບໄປເລີຍ
    if (!photoData) {
      return '';
    }
    
    if (photoData.startsWith('https://drive.google.com')) {
      Logger.log('Photo already uploaded, reusing URL: ' + photoData);
      return photoData; // ໃຊ້ URL ເດີມ ບໍ່ອັບໂຫລດຊ້ຳ
    }
    
    if (!photoData.startsWith('data:image')) {
      return '';
    }
    
    const base64Data = photoData.split(',')[1];
    const blob = Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(base64Data), 'image/png', fileName);
    const folder = DriveApp.getFolderById(PHOTO_FOLDER_ID);
    
    // ກວດສອບວ່າມີໄຟລ໌ຊື່ນີ້ຢູ່ແລ້ວຫຼືບໍ່
    const existingFiles = folder.getFilesByName(fileName);
    if (existingFiles.hasNext()) {
      const existingFile = existingFiles.next();
      Logger.log('File exists, reusing URL: ' + existingFile.getUrl());
      return existingFile.getUrl(); // ໃຊ້ URL ເດີມ
    }
    
    // ອັບໂຫລດໃໝ່
    const file = folder.createFile(blob);
    file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
    
    Logger.log('New file uploaded: ' + file.getUrl());
    return file.getUrl();
    
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error uploading photo: ' + error.message);
    return photoData;
  }
}

function getLoginData() {
  try {
    Logger.log('=== Starting getLoginData ===');
    const startTime = new Date().getTime();
    
    const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
    const cacheKey = 'loginData_v5';
    const cachedData = cache.get(cacheKey);
    
    if (cachedData) {
      Logger.log('✅ Using cached login data (instant load)');
      const endTime = new Date().getTime();
      Logger.log('Time taken: ' + (endTime - startTime) + ' ms');
      return JSON.parse(cachedData);
    }
    
    Logger.log('📥 Fetching fresh data from sheet...');
    Logger.log('Using LOGIN Sheet ID: ' + SHEET_IDS.LOGIN);
    
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_IDS.LOGIN);
    
    // ===== ✅ ພາກທີ 1: ດຶງລາຍຊື່ ແຂວງ/ເມືອງ/ບ້ານ ຈາກຕາຕະລາງ "village" (ຕົວຈິງ 48 ບ້ານ) =====
    const villageSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES.VILLAGE);
    if (!villageSheet) {
      throw new Error('Sheet not found: ' + SHEET_NAMES.VILLAGE);
    }
    
    const villageLastRow = villageSheet.getLastRow();
    const provinces = new Set();
    const districts = {};
    const villages = {};
    
    if (villageLastRow >= 2) {
      // ອ່ານຖັນ B-D (village, district, province) ຕາມທີ່ validateLogin ໃຊ້
      const villageData = villageSheet.getRange(2, 2, villageLastRow - 1, 3).getValues(); // B,C,D
      
      villageData.forEach(function(row) {
        const village = row[0] ? String(row[0]).trim() : '';
        const district = row[1] ? String(row[1]).trim() : '';
        const province = row[2] ? String(row[2]).trim() : '';
        
        if (!village || !district || !province) {
          return;
        }
        
        provinces.add(province);
        
        if (!districts[province]) {
          districts[province] = new Set();
        }
        districts[province].add(district);
        
        const key = province + '|' + district;
        if (!villages[key]) {
          villages[key] = new Set();
        }
        villages[key].add(village);
      });
    }
    
    Logger.log('✅ Villages loaded from master "village" sheet: ' + villageLastRow + ' rows');
    
    // ===== ພາກທີ 2: ດຶງຂໍ້ມູນຜູ້ໃຊ້ (ອສບ) ຈາກຕາຕະລາງ "ປະຫວັດ ອສບ" ຄືເດີມ ສຳລັບ login step ຕໍ່ໄປ =====
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES.USER);
    
    if (!sheet) {
      throw new Error('Sheet not found: ' + SHEET_NAMES.USER);
    }
    
    const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
    Logger.log('Last row: ' + lastRow);
    
    const users = [];
    
    if (lastRow >= 2) {
      const data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 20).getValues();
      Logger.log('User data retrieved: ' + data.length + ' rows');
      
      data.forEach(function(row) {
        const code = row[0] ? String(row[0]).trim() : '';
        const name = row[1] ? String(row[1]).trim() : '';
        const phone = row[8] ? String(row[8]).trim() : '';
        const village = row[4] ? String(row[4]).trim() : '';
        const district = row[18] ? String(row[18]).trim() : '';
        const province = row[19] ? String(row[19]).trim() : '';
        
        if (!code || !name || !village || !district || !province) {
          return;
        }
        
        users.push({
          code: code,
          name: name,
          phone: phone,
          village: village,
          district: district,
          province: province
        });
      });
    }
    
    const result = {
      provinces: Array.from(provinces).sort(),
      districts: {},
      villages: {},
      users: users
    };
    
    Object.keys(districts).forEach(function(province) {
      result.districts[province] = Array.from(districts[province]).sort();
    });
    
    Object.keys(villages).forEach(function(key) {
      result.villages[key] = Array.from(villages[key]).sort();
    });
    
    const endTime = new Date().getTime();
    Logger.log('✅ Login data loaded successfully');
    Logger.log('Provinces: ' + result.provinces.length);
    Logger.log('Users: ' + result.users.length);
    Logger.log('Time taken: ' + (endTime - startTime) + ' ms');
    
    try {
      const jsonString = JSON.stringify(result);
      const maxSize = 100000;
      
      if (jsonString.length < maxSize) {
        cache.put(cacheKey, jsonString, 600);
        Logger.log('✅ Login data cached for 10 minutes');
      } else {
        Logger.log('⚠️ Data too large for cache: ' + jsonString.length + ' bytes');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      Logger.log('⚠️ Cache error: ' + e.message);
    }
    
    return result;
    
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('❌ ERROR in getLoginData: ' + error.message);
    Logger.log('Stack: ' + error.stack);
    throw new Error('Error loading login data: ' + error.message);
  }
}

// ===================================
// Validate Login & Get Village Sheet ID
// ===================================
function validateLogin(loginData) {
  try {
    Logger.log('=== Validating Login ===');
    Logger.log('Input: ' + JSON.stringify(loginData));
    
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_IDS.LOGIN);
    Logger.log('LOGIN sheet opened');
    
    const villageSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES.VILLAGE);
    Logger.log('Village sheet: ' + (villageSheet ? 'found' : 'NOT FOUND'));
    
    if (!villageSheet) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Village sheet not found in LOGIN'
      };
    }
    
    const villageData = villageSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    Logger.log('Village data rows: ' + villageData.length);
    
    const inputVillage = String(loginData.village).trim();
    const inputDistrict = String(loginData.district).trim();
    const inputProvince = String(loginData.province).trim();
    
    Logger.log('Looking for: V=' + inputVillage + ', D=' + inputDistrict + ', P=' + inputProvince);
    
    // Search in village sheet
    // Column B = village (index 1)
    // Column C = district (index 2)
    // Column D = province (index 3)
    // Column E = Sheet ID (index 4)
    
    for (let i = 1; i < villageData.length; i++) {
      const row = villageData[i];
      
      const rowVillage = row[1] ? String(row[1]).trim() : '';
      const rowDistrict = row[2] ? String(row[2]).trim() : '';
      const rowProvince = row[3] ? String(row[3]).trim() : '';
      const sheetId = row[4] ? String(row[4]).trim() : '';
      
      Logger.log('Row ' + i + ': V=' + rowVillage + ', D=' + rowDistrict + ', P=' + rowProvince + ', ID=' + sheetId);
      
      if (rowVillage === inputVillage && rowDistrict === inputDistrict && rowProvince === inputProvince) {
        Logger.log('✅ MATCH FOUND! Sheet ID: ' + sheetId);
        
        return {
          success: true,
          villageSheetId: sheetId,
          village: inputVillage,
          district: inputDistrict,
          province: inputProvince
        };
      }
    }
    
    Logger.log('❌ No match found');
    return {
      success: false,
      message: 'Village not found in village sheet'
    };
    
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('ERROR in validateLogin: ' + error.message);
    Logger.log('Stack: ' + error.stack);
    return {
      success: false,
      message: 'Error: ' + error.message
    };
  }
}

// ===================================
// Get Options Data
// ===================================
function getOptionsData() {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_IDS.OPTIONS);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES.OPTIONS);
    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    
    const options = {};
    const headers = data[0];
    
    for (let col = 0; col < headers.length; col++) {
      const columnData = [];
      for (let row = 1; row < data.length; row++) {
        if (data[row][col]) {
          columnData.push(data[row][col]);
        }
      }
      options[String.fromCharCode(65 + col)] = columnData;
    }
    
    return options;
  } catch (error) {
    throw new Error('Error loading options: ' + error.message);
  }
}
// ===================================
// Helper: Format Birth Date (ແກ້ໄຂບັນຫາ Google Sheets ປ່ຽນຂໍ້ຄວາມວັນທີເປັນ Date ອັດຕະໂນມັດ)
// ===================================
function formatBirthDate(value) {
  if (!value) return '';
  
  if (value instanceof Date) {
    const day = value.getDate();
    const month = value.getMonth() + 1;
    const year = value.getFullYear();
    return day + '/' + month + '/' + year;
  }
  
  return String(value).trim();
}
// ===================================
// Get Household Data
// ===================================
function getHouseholdData(villageSheetId) {
  try {
    Logger.log('=== Getting household data ===');
    Logger.log('Village Sheet ID: ' + villageSheetId);
    
    if (!villageSheetId) {
      throw new Error('No village sheet ID');
    }
    
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(villageSheetId);
    const householdSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES.HOUSEHOLD);
    const memberSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES.MEMBER);
    
    if (!householdSheet || !memberSheet) {
      Logger.log('Sheets not found');
      return [];
    }
    
    const householdLastRow = householdSheet.getLastRow();
    const memberLastRow = memberSheet.getLastRow();
    
    if (householdLastRow < 2) {
      return [];
    }
    
  const householdData = householdSheet.getRange(1, 1, householdLastRow, 22).getValues();
    let memberData = [[]];
    if (memberLastRow >= 2) {
      memberData = memberSheet.getRange(1, 1, memberLastRow, 23).getValues();
    }
    
    const households = [];
    
    for (let i = 1; i < householdData.length; i++) {
      const row = householdData[i];
      const householdCode = row[0] ? String(row[0]).trim() : '';
      
      if (!householdCode) {
        continue;
      }
      
      const members = [];
      if (memberData.length > 1) {
        for (let j = 1; j < memberData.length; j++) {
          const memberHouseholdCode = memberData[j][0] ? String(memberData[j][0]).trim() : '';
          
          if (memberHouseholdCode === householdCode) {
            members.push({
              householdCode: String(memberData[j][0] || ''),
              memberCode: String(memberData[j][1] || ''),
              name: String(memberData[j][2] || ''),
              photo: String(memberData[j][3] || ''),
              gender: String(memberData[j][4] || ''),
             birthDate: formatBirthDate(memberData[j][5]),
              inRegistry: String(memberData[j][6] || ''),
              bodyComplete: String(memberData[j][7] || ''),
              relationship: String(memberData[j][8] || ''),
              ethnicity: String(memberData[j][9] || ''),
              occupation: String(memberData[j][10] || ''),
              disease: String(memberData[j][11] || ''),
              insurance: String(memberData[j][12] || ''),
              phone: String(memberData[j][13] || ''),
              education: String(memberData[j][14] || ''),
              religion: String(memberData[j][15] || ''),
              maritalStatus: String(memberData[j][16] || ''),
              actualResident: String(memberData[j][17] || ''),
              village: String(memberData[j][18] || ''),
              district: String(memberData[j][19] || ''),
              province: String(memberData[j][20] || ''),
              unit: String(memberData[j][21] || ''),
              housePosition: String(memberData[j][22] || '')
            });
          }
        }
      }
      
     households.push({
  householdCode: String(row[0] || ''),
  name: String(row[1] || ''),
  photo: String(row[2] || ''),
  phone: String(row[3] || ''),
  houseNumber: String(row[4] || ''),
  roofCount: String(row[5] || ''),
  householdCount: String(row[6] || ''),
  familyCount: String(row[7] || ''),
  registryBookCount: String(row[8] || ''),
  registryNumber: String(row[9] || ''),
  familyMemberCount: String(row[10] || ''),
  pageCount: String(row[11] || ''),
  unit: String(row[12] || ''),
  rentalLocation: String(row[13] || ''),
  monthlyIncome: String(row[14] || ''),
  housePosition: String(row[15] || ''),
  village: String(row[16] || ''),
  district: String(row[17] || ''),
  province: String(row[18] || ''),
  userCode: String(row[19] || ''),
  userName: String(row[20] || ''),
  villageParticipation: String(row[21] || ''), // ເພີ່ມແຖວນີ້
  members: members
});
    }
    
    Logger.log('Households loaded: ' + households.length);
    return households;
    
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('ERROR in getHouseholdData: ' + error.message);
    return [];
  }
}

// ===================================
// Save Household
// ===================================
function saveHousehold(householdData, villageSheetId) {
  try {
    Logger.log('Saving household to: ' + villageSheetId);
    
    if (!villageSheetId) {
      throw new Error('No village sheet ID');
    }
    
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(villageSheetId);
    const householdSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES.HOUSEHOLD);
    const memberSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES.MEMBER);
    
    if (!householdSheet || !memberSheet) {
      throw new Error('Sheets not found');
    }
    
    if (!householdData.householdCode) {
      householdData.householdCode = 'Data-' + generateCode(13);
    }
    
    let householdPhotoUrl = householdData.photo || '';
    if (householdData.photo && householdData.photo.startsWith('data:image')) {
      const photoFileName = 'household_' + householdData.householdCode + '_' + new Date().getTime() + '.png';
      householdPhotoUrl = uploadPhoto(householdData.photo, photoFileName);
    }
    
   const householdRow = [
  householdData.householdCode,
  householdData.name,
  householdPhotoUrl,
  householdData.phone || '',
  householdData.houseNumber || '',
  householdData.roofCount || '',
  householdData.householdCount || '',
  householdData.familyCount || '',
  householdData.registryBookCount || '',
  householdData.registryNumber || '',
  householdData.familyMemberCount || '',
  householdData.pageCount || '',
  householdData.unit || '',
  householdData.rentalLocation || '',
  householdData.monthlyIncome || '',
  householdData.housePosition || '',
  householdData.village || '',
  householdData.district || '',
  householdData.province || '',
  householdData.userCode || '',
  householdData.userName || '',
  householdData.villageParticipation || '' // ເພີ່ມແຖວນີ້
];
    
    householdSheet.appendRow(householdRow);
    
    if (householdData.members && householdData.members.length > 0) {
      householdData.members.forEach(function(member) {
        if (!member.memberCode) {
          member.memberCode = 'Data-' + generateCode(13);
        }
        
        let memberPhotoUrl = member.photo || '';
        if (member.photo && member.photo.startsWith('data:image')) {
          const photoFileName = 'member_' + member.memberCode + '_' + new Date().getTime() + '.png';
          memberPhotoUrl = uploadPhoto(member.photo, photoFileName);
        }
        
        const memberRow = [
          householdData.householdCode,
          member.memberCode,
          member.name || '',
          memberPhotoUrl,
          member.gender || '',
          member.birthDate || '',
          member.inRegistry || '',
          member.bodyComplete || '',
          member.relationship || '',
          member.ethnicity || '',
          member.occupation || '',
          member.disease || '',
          member.insurance || '',
          member.phone || '',
          member.education || '',
          member.religion || '',
          member.maritalStatus || '',
          member.actualResident || '',
          householdData.village || '',
          householdData.district || '',
          householdData.province || '',
          householdData.unit || '',
          householdData.housePosition || ''
        ];
        
        memberSheet.appendRow(memberRow);
      });
    }
    
  return {
      success: true,
      householdCode: householdData.householdCode,
      household: householdData,
      message: 'Saved successfully'
    };
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('ERROR in saveHousehold: ' + error.message);
    return {
      success: false,
      message: 'Error: ' + error.message
    };
  }
}
function updateHousehold(householdData, villageSheetId) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  
  try {
    lock.waitLock(30000);
  } catch (lockError) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: 'ລະບົບກຳລັງບັນທຶກຂໍ້ມູນຄົນອື່ນຢູ່ ກະລຸນາລໍຖ້າ 10 ວິນາທີແລ້ວກົດບັນທຶກໃໝ່'
    };
  }
  
  try {
    if (!villageSheetId) {
      throw new Error('No village sheet ID');
    }
    
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(villageSheetId);
    const householdSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES.HOUSEHOLD);
    const memberSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES.MEMBER);
    
    if (!householdSheet || !memberSheet) {
      throw new Error('Sheets not found');
    }
    
    let householdPhotoUrl = householdData.photo || '';
    if (householdData.photo && householdData.photo.startsWith('data:image')) {
      const photoFileName = 'household_' + householdData.householdCode + '_' + new Date().getTime() + '.png';
      householdPhotoUrl = uploadPhoto(householdData.photo, photoFileName);
    }
    
    const householdValues = householdSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let i = 1; i < householdValues.length; i++) {
      if (householdValues[i][0] === householdData.householdCode) {
       householdSheet.getRange(i + 1, 1, 1, 22).setValues([[
  householdData.householdCode,
  householdData.name,
  householdPhotoUrl,
  householdData.phone || '',
  householdData.houseNumber || '',
  householdData.roofCount || '',
  householdData.householdCount || '',
  householdData.familyCount || '',
  householdData.registryBookCount || '',
  householdData.registryNumber || '',
  householdData.familyMemberCount || '',
  householdData.pageCount || '',
  householdData.unit || '',
  householdData.rentalLocation || '',
  householdData.monthlyIncome || '',
  householdData.housePosition || '',
  householdData.village || '',
  householdData.district || '',
  householdData.province || '',
  householdData.userCode || '',
  householdData.userName || '',
  householdData.villageParticipation || ''
]]);
        break;
      }
    }
    
    const memberValues = memberSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let i = memberValues.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (memberValues[i][0] === householdData.householdCode) {
        memberSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }
    
    if (householdData.members && householdData.members.length > 0) {
      householdData.members.forEach(function(member) {
        if (!member.memberCode) {
          member.memberCode = 'Data-' + generateCode(13);
        }
        
        let memberPhotoUrl = member.photo || '';
        if (member.photo && member.photo.startsWith('data:image')) {
          const photoFileName = 'member_' + member.memberCode + '_' + new Date().getTime() + '.png';
          memberPhotoUrl = uploadPhoto(member.photo, photoFileName);
        }
        
        const memberRow = [
          householdData.householdCode,
          member.memberCode,
          member.name || '',
          memberPhotoUrl,
          member.gender || '',
          member.birthDate || '',
          member.inRegistry || '',
          member.bodyComplete || '',
          member.relationship || '',
          member.ethnicity || '',
          member.occupation || '',
          member.disease || '',
          member.insurance || '',
          member.phone || '',
          member.education || '',
          member.religion || '',
          member.maritalStatus || '',
          member.actualResident || '',
          householdData.village || '',
          householdData.district || '',
          householdData.province || '',
          householdData.unit || '',
          householdData.housePosition || ''
        ];
        
        memberSheet.appendRow(memberRow);
      });
    }
    
 return {
      success: true,
      household: householdData,
      message: 'Updated successfully'
    };
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('ERROR in updateHousehold: ' + error.message);
    return {
      success: false,
      message: 'Error: ' + error.message
    };
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

// ===================================
// Delete Member
// ===================================
function deleteMember(memberCode, reason, villageSheetId) {
  try {
    if (!villageSheetId) {
      throw new Error('No village sheet ID');
    }
    
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(villageSheetId);
    const memberSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES.MEMBER);
    const values = memberSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    
    for (let i = 1; i < values.length; i++) {
      if (values[i][1] === memberCode) {
        const photoUrl = values[i][3];
        
        if (reason === 'ຂໍ້ມູນບໍ່ຖືກຕ້ອງ' || reason === 'ເສຍຊີວິດ') {
          deletePhotoFromDrive(photoUrl);
        }
        
        memberSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
        logDeletion('member', memberCode, reason, villageSheetId);
        
        return {
          success: true,
          message: 'Deleted successfully'
        };
      }
    }
    
    return {
      success: false,
      message: 'Not found'
    };
  } catch (error) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: 'Error: ' + error.message
    };
  }
}

// ===================================
// Delete Household
// ===================================
function deleteHousehold(householdCode, reason, villageSheetId) {
  try {
    if (!villageSheetId) {
      throw new Error('No village sheet ID');
    }
    
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(villageSheetId);
    const householdSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES.HOUSEHOLD);
    const memberSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES.MEMBER);
    
    if (reason === 'ຂໍ້ມູນບໍ່ຖືກຕ້ອງ') {
      const householdValues = householdSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      for (let i = 1; i < householdValues.length; i++) {
        if (householdValues[i][0] === householdCode) {
          const photoUrl = householdValues[i][2];
          deletePhotoFromDrive(photoUrl);
          break;
        }
      }
      
      const memberValues = memberSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      for (let i = 1; i < memberValues.length; i++) {
        if (memberValues[i][0] === householdCode) {
          const photoUrl = memberValues[i][3];
          deletePhotoFromDrive(photoUrl);
        }
      }
    }
    
    const householdValues = householdSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let i = householdValues.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (householdValues[i][0] === householdCode) {
        householdSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
        break;
      }
    }
    
    const memberValues = memberSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let i = memberValues.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (memberValues[i][0] === householdCode) {
        memberSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }
    
    logDeletion('household', householdCode, reason, villageSheetId);
    
    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Deleted successfully'
    };
  } catch (error) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: 'Error: ' + error.message
    };
  }
}

// ===================================
// Helper Functions
// ===================================
function generateCode(length) {
  let code = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
    code += Math.floor(Math.random() * 10);
  }
  return code;
}

function deletePhotoFromDrive(photoUrl) {
  try {
    if (!photoUrl || !photoUrl.includes('drive.google.com')) {
      return;
    }
    
    const match = photoUrl.match(/[-\w]{25,}/);
    if (!match) {
      return;
    }
    
    const fileId = match[0];
    const file = DriveApp.getFileById(fileId);
    
    if (file) {
      file.setTrashed(true);
    }
    
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error deleting photo: ' + error.message);
  }
}

function logDeletion(type, code, reason, villageSheetId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(villageSheetId);
    let logSheet = ss.getSheetByName('ປະຫວັດການລົບ');
    
    if (!logSheet) {
      logSheet = ss.insertSheet('ປະຫວັດການລົບ');
      logSheet.appendRow(['ວັນທີ', 'ປະເພດ', 'ລະຫັດ', 'ເຫດຜົນ', 'ຜູ້ລົບ']);
    }
    
    logSheet.appendRow([
      new Date(),
      type,
      code,
      reason,
      Session.getActiveUser().getEmail()
    ]);
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error logging deletion: ' + error.message);
  }
}
function searchHouseholds(searchQuery, searchField, villageSheetId) {
  try {
    if (!villageSheetId) {
      throw new Error('No village sheet ID');
    }
    
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(villageSheetId);
    const householdSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES.HOUSEHOLD);
    const memberSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES.MEMBER);
    
    if (!householdSheet) {
      return [];
    }
    
    const householdData = householdSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const memberData = memberSheet ? memberSheet.getDataRange().getValues() : [];
    const results = [];
    const addedHouseholds = new Set(); // ປ້ອງກັນຊ້ຳ
    
    const searchLower = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
    
    // ຄົ້ນຫາຈາກຫົວໜ້າຄອບຄົວ
    for (let i = 1; i < householdData.length; i++) {
      const row = householdData[i];
      let match = false;
      
      if (searchField === 'name') {
        match = row[1] && String(row[1]).toLowerCase().includes(searchLower);
      } else if (searchField === 'phone') {
        match = row[3] && String(row[3]).includes(searchQuery);
      } else if (searchField === 'householdCode') {
        match = row[0] && String(row[0]).includes(searchQuery);
      } else if (searchField === 'houseNumber') {
        match = row[4] && String(row[4]).toLowerCase().includes(searchLower);
      } else {
        // ຄົ້ນຫາທຸກຟິລດ໌
        match = (row[0] && String(row[0]).toLowerCase().includes(searchLower)) ||
                (row[1] && String(row[1]).toLowerCase().includes(searchLower)) ||
                (row[3] && String(row[3]).includes(searchQuery)) ||
                (row[4] && String(row[4]).toLowerCase().includes(searchLower));
      }
      
      if (match) {
        const householdCode = String(row[0]);
        if (!addedHouseholds.has(householdCode)) {
          results.push({
            householdCode: householdCode,
            name: String(row[1] || ''),
            photo: String(row[2] || ''),
            phone: String(row[3] || ''),
            houseNumber: String(row[4] || ''),
            roofCount: String(row[5] || ''),
            householdCount: String(row[6] || ''),
            familyCount: String(row[7] || ''),
            registryBookCount: String(row[8] || ''),
            registryNumber: String(row[9] || ''),
            familyMemberCount: String(row[10] || ''),
            pageCount: String(row[11] || ''),
            unit: String(row[12] || ''),
            rentalLocation: String(row[13] || ''),
            monthlyIncome: String(row[14] || ''),
            housePosition: String(row[15] || ''),
            village: String(row[16] || ''),
            district: String(row[17] || ''),
            province: String(row[18] || ''),
            userCode: String(row[19] || ''),
            userName: String(row[20] || '')
          });
          addedHouseholds.add(householdCode);
        }
      }
    }
    
    // ຄົ້ນຫາຈາກສະມາຊິກ
    if (memberData.length > 1) {
      for (let i = 1; i < memberData.length; i++) {
        const row = memberData[i];
        const householdCode = String(row[0]);
        
        if (addedHouseholds.has(householdCode)) {
          continue; // ຖ້າພົບແລ້ວຂ້າມ
        }
        
        let match = false;
        
        if (searchField === 'name') {
          match = row[2] && String(row[2]).toLowerCase().includes(searchLower);
        } else if (searchField === 'phone') {
          match = row[13] && String(row[13]).includes(searchQuery);
        } else {
          // ຄົ້ນຫາທຸກຟິລດ໌ຂອງສະມາຊິກ
          match = (row[2] && String(row[2]).toLowerCase().includes(searchLower)) ||
                  (row[13] && String(row[13]).includes(searchQuery));
        }
        
        if (match) {
          // ດຶງຂໍ້ມູນຄອບຄົວຂອງສະມາຊິກຄົນນີ້
          const householdRow = householdData.find(h => String(h[0]) === householdCode);
          if (householdRow) {
            results.push({
              householdCode: householdCode,
              name: String(householdRow[1] || ''),
              photo: String(householdRow[2] || ''),
              phone: String(householdRow[3] || ''),
              houseNumber: String(householdRow[4] || ''),
              roofCount: String(householdRow[5] || ''),
              householdCount: String(householdRow[6] || ''),
              familyCount: String(householdRow[7] || ''),
              registryBookCount: String(householdRow[8] || ''),
              registryNumber: String(householdRow[9] || ''),
              familyMemberCount: String(householdRow[10] || ''),
              pageCount: String(householdRow[11] || ''),
              unit: String(householdRow[12] || ''),
              rentalLocation: String(householdRow[13] || ''),
              monthlyIncome: String(householdRow[14] || ''),
              housePosition: String(householdRow[15] || ''),
              village: String(householdRow[16] || ''),
              district: String(householdRow[17] || ''),
              province: String(householdRow[18] || ''),
              userCode: String(householdRow[19] || ''),
              userName: String(householdRow[20] || '')
            });
            addedHouseholds.add(householdCode);
          }
        }
      }
    }
    
    Logger.log('Search found: ' + results.length + ' households');
    return results;
    
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('ERROR in searchHouseholds: ' + error.message);
    return [];
  }
}

// ===================================
// MOBILE POPULATION - NEW FUNCTIONS
// ===================================

const SHEET_NAMES_MOBILE = {
  MOBILE: 'ປະຊາກອນເຄື່ອນທີ່'
};

// ===================================
// Get Mobile Population Data
// ===================================
function getMobileData(villageSheetId) {
  try {
    Logger.log('=== Getting mobile population data ===');
    Logger.log('Village Sheet ID: ' + villageSheetId);
    
    if (!villageSheetId) {
      throw new Error('No village sheet ID');
    }
    
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(villageSheetId);
    const mobileSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES_MOBILE.MOBILE);
    
    if (!mobileSheet) {
      Logger.log('Mobile sheet not found');
      return [];
    }
    
    const lastRow = mobileSheet.getLastRow();
    if (lastRow < 2) {
      return [];
    }
    
    const mobileData = mobileSheet.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, 28).getValues(); // A-AB (28 columns)
    const mobiles = [];
    
    for (let i = 1; i < mobileData.length; i++) {
      const row = mobileData[i];
      const mobileCode = row[0] ? String(row[0]).trim() : '';
      
      if (!mobileCode) {
        continue;
      }
      
      // Parse rental periods (Column Z-AA)
      const rentalPeriods = parseRentalPeriods(row[25], row[26]); // Z, AA
      
      mobiles.push({
        mobileCode: String(row[0] || ''),           // A - NUMBER
        householdCode: String(row[1] || ''),        // B - ລະຫັດຫ້ອງແຖວ
        province: String(row[2] || ''),             // C - ແຂວງປະຈຸບັນ
        district: String(row[3] || ''),             // D - ເມືອງປະຈຸບັນ
        village: String(row[4] || ''),              // E - ບ້ານຢູ່ປະຈຸບັນ
        unit: String(row[5] || ''),                 // F - ໜ່ວຍູ່ປະຈຸບັນ
        position: String(row[6] || ''),             // G - ຕໍາແໜ່ງບ່ອນພັກ
        roomNumber: String(row[7] || ''),           // H - ຫ້ອງເບີທີ
        roomCount: String(row[8] || ''),            // I - ຈໍານວນຫ້ອງມີຄົນຢູ່
        name: String(row[9] || ''),                 // J - ຊື່ ແລະ ນາມສະກຸນ
        birthDate: String(row[10] || ''),           // K - ວັນເດືອນປີເກີດ
        gender: String(row[11] || ''),              // L - ເພດ
        nationality: String(row[12] || ''),         // M - ສັນຊາດ
        ethnicity: String(row[13] || ''),           // N - ຊົນເຜົ່າ
        religion: String(row[14] || ''),            // O - ສາສະໜາ
        education: String(row[15] || ''),           // P - ລະດັບການສຶກສາ
        relationship: String(row[16] || ''),        // Q - ສາຍພົວພັນຄອບຄົວ
        maritalStatus: String(row[17] || ''),       // R - ສະຖານະພາບຄອບຄົວ
        phone: String(row[18] || ''),               // S - ເບີໂທຜູ້ກ່ຽວ
        occupation: String(row[19] || ''),          // T - ອາຊີບ
        workplace: String(row[20] || ''),           // U - ລະບຸບ່ອນເຮັດວຽກ
        purpose: String(row[21] || ''),             // V - ຈຸດປະສົງໃນການພັກເຊົ່າ
        photo: String(row[22] || ''),               // W - ຮູບປະຈໍາຕົວ
        hasPermit: String(row[23] || ''),           // X - ປື້ມອະນຸຍາດພັກເຊົ່າຊົ່ວຄາວ
        permitNumber: String(row[23] || '').includes('ມີ') && row[23].includes(':') ? 
                     row[23].split(':')[1].trim() : '',
        firstArrival: String(row[24] || ''),        // Y - ວັນທີມາພັກເຊົ່າຄັ້ງທໍາອິດ
        rentalPeriods: rentalPeriods,               // Z-AA parsed
        ownerName: String(row[27] || ''),           // AB - ຊື່ເຈົ້າຂອງຫ້ອງແຖວ
        rentalType: row[1] ? 'ພັກເຊົາຄົງທີ່' : 'ພັກເຊົາເຄື່ອນທີ່' // ເບິ່ງວ່າມີ householdCode ຫຼືບໍ່
      });
    }
    
    Logger.log('Mobiles loaded: ' + mobiles.length);
    return mobiles;
    
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('ERROR in getMobileData: ' + error.message);
    return [];
  }
}

// ===================================
// Parse Rental Periods (Z-AA)
// ===================================
function parseRentalPeriods(renewalData, endDateData) {
  const periods = [];
  
  if (!renewalData || !endDateData) {
    return periods;
  }
  
  // Parse format: "ຄັ້ງທີ 1: 01/01/2025 (30 ວັນ) | ຄັ້ງທີ 2: 01/02/2025 (60 ວັນ)"
  const renewalStr = String(renewalData);
  const endDateStr = String(endDateData);
  
  const renewalParts = renewalStr.split('|');
  const endDateParts = endDateStr.split('|');
  
  for (let i = 0; i < renewalParts.length; i++) {
    const renewal = renewalParts[i].trim();
    const endDate = endDateParts[i] ? endDateParts[i].trim() : '';
    
    // Extract date and days
    const dateMatch = renewal.match(/(\d{2}\/\d{2}\/\d{4})/);
    const daysMatch = renewal.match(/\((\d+)\s*[^\)]*\)/);
    
    if (dateMatch) {
      periods.push({
        periodNumber: i + 1,
        renewalDate: dateMatch[1],
        days: daysMatch ? parseInt(daysMatch[1]) : 0,
        endDate: endDate
      });
    }
  }
  
  return periods;
}

// ===================================
// Save Mobile Population
// ===================================
function saveMobile(mobileData, villageSheetId) {
  try {
    Logger.log('Saving mobile to: ' + villageSheetId);
    
    if (!villageSheetId) {
      throw new Error('No village sheet ID');
    }
    
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(villageSheetId);
    const mobileSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES_MOBILE.MOBILE);
    
    if (!mobileSheet) {
      throw new Error('Mobile sheet not found');
    }
    
    if (!mobileData.mobileCode) {
      mobileData.mobileCode = 'Mobile-' + generateCode(13);
    }
    
    // Upload photo if needed
    let photoUrl = mobileData.photo || '';
    if (mobileData.photo && mobileData.photo.startsWith('data:image')) {
      const photoFileName = 'mobile_' + mobileData.mobileCode + '_' + new Date().getTime() + '.png';
      photoUrl = uploadPhoto(mobileData.photo, photoFileName);
    }
    
    // Format permit
    let permitData = mobileData.hasPermit || 'ບໍ່ມີ';
    if (mobileData.hasPermit === 'ມີ' && mobileData.permitNumber) {
      permitData = 'ມີ: ' + mobileData.permitNumber;
    }
    
    // Format rental periods
    const rentalPeriodsStr = formatRentalPeriods(mobileData.rentalPeriods);
    const endDatesStr = formatEndDates(mobileData.rentalPeriods);
    
    const mobileRow = [
      mobileData.mobileCode,           // A
      mobileData.householdCode || '',  // B
      mobileData.province || '',       // C
      mobileData.district || '',       // D
      mobileData.village || '',        // E
      mobileData.unit || '',           // F
      mobileData.position || '',       // G
      mobileData.roomNumber || '',     // H
      mobileData.roomCount || '',      // I
      mobileData.name || '',           // J
      mobileData.birthDate || '',      // K
      mobileData.gender || '',         // L
      mobileData.nationality || '',    // M
      mobileData.ethnicity || '',      // N
      mobileData.religion || '',       // O
      mobileData.education || '',      // P
      mobileData.relationship || '',   // Q
      mobileData.maritalStatus || '',  // R
      mobileData.phone || '',          // S
      mobileData.occupation || '',     // T
      mobileData.workplace || '',      // U
      mobileData.purpose || '',        // V
      photoUrl,                        // W
      permitData,                      // X
      mobileData.firstArrival || '',   // Y
      rentalPeriodsStr,                // Z
      endDatesStr,                     // AA
      mobileData.ownerName || ''       // AB
    ];
    
    mobileSheet.appendRow(mobileRow);
    
    return {
      success: true,
      mobileCode: mobileData.mobileCode,
      message: 'Saved successfully'
    };
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('ERROR in saveMobile: ' + error.message);
    return {
      success: false,
      message: 'Error: ' + error.message
    };
  }
}

// ===================================
// Update Mobile Population
// ===================================
function updateMobile(mobileData, villageSheetId) {
  try {
    if (!villageSheetId) {
      throw new Error('No village sheet ID');
    }
    
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(villageSheetId);
    const mobileSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES_MOBILE.MOBILE);
    
    if (!mobileSheet) {
      throw new Error('Mobile sheet not found');
    }
    
    // Upload photo if needed
    let photoUrl = mobileData.photo || '';
    if (mobileData.photo && mobileData.photo.startsWith('data:image')) {
      const photoFileName = 'mobile_' + mobileData.mobileCode + '_' + new Date().getTime() + '.png';
      photoUrl = uploadPhoto(mobileData.photo, photoFileName);
    }
    
    // Format permit
    let permitData = mobileData.hasPermit || 'ບໍ່ມີ';
    if (mobileData.hasPermit === 'ມີ' && mobileData.permitNumber) {
      permitData = 'ມີ: ' + mobileData.permitNumber;
    }
    
    // Format rental periods
    const rentalPeriodsStr = formatRentalPeriods(mobileData.rentalPeriods);
    const endDatesStr = formatEndDates(mobileData.rentalPeriods);
    
    const values = mobileSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let i = 1; i < values.length; i++) {
      if (values[i][0] === mobileData.mobileCode) {
        mobileSheet.getRange(i + 1, 1, 1, 28).setValues([[
          mobileData.mobileCode,
          mobileData.householdCode || '',
          mobileData.province || '',
          mobileData.district || '',
          mobileData.village || '',
          mobileData.unit || '',
          mobileData.position || '',
          mobileData.roomNumber || '',
          mobileData.roomCount || '',
          mobileData.name || '',
          mobileData.birthDate || '',
          mobileData.gender || '',
          mobileData.nationality || '',
          mobileData.ethnicity || '',
          mobileData.religion || '',
          mobileData.education || '',
          mobileData.relationship || '',
          mobileData.maritalStatus || '',
          mobileData.phone || '',
          mobileData.occupation || '',
          mobileData.workplace || '',
          mobileData.purpose || '',
          photoUrl,
          permitData,
          mobileData.firstArrival || '',
          rentalPeriodsStr,
          endDatesStr,
          mobileData.ownerName || ''
        ]]);
        break;
      }
    }
    
    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Updated successfully'
    };
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('ERROR in updateMobile: ' + error.message);
    return {
      success: false,
      message: 'Error: ' + error.message
    };
  }
}

// ===================================
// Delete Mobile
// ===================================
function deleteMobile(mobileCode, reason, villageSheetId) {
  try {
    if (!villageSheetId) {
      throw new Error('No village sheet ID');
    }
    
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(villageSheetId);
    const mobileSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES_MOBILE.MOBILE);
    const values = mobileSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    
    for (let i = 1; i < values.length; i++) {
      if (values[i][0] === mobileCode) {
        const photoUrl = values[i][22]; // Column W
        
        if (reason === 'ຂໍ້ມູນບໍ່ຖືກຕ້ອງ') {
          deletePhotoFromDrive(photoUrl);
        }
        
        mobileSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
        logDeletion('mobile', mobileCode, reason, villageSheetId);
        
        return {
          success: true,
          message: 'Deleted successfully'
        };
      }
    }
    
    return {
      success: false,
      message: 'Not found'
    };
  } catch (error) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: 'Error: ' + error.message
    };
  }
}

// ===================================
// Search Mobile Population
// ===================================
function searchMobile(searchQuery, villageSheetId) {
  try {
    if (!villageSheetId) {
      throw new Error('No village sheet ID');
    }
    
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(villageSheetId);
    const mobileSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES_MOBILE.MOBILE);
    
    if (!mobileSheet) {
      return [];
    }
    
    const data = mobileSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const results = [];
    const searchLower = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
    
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      const row = data[i];
      
      // Search in: name, phone, room number, owner name
      const match = (row[9] && String(row[9]).toLowerCase().includes(searchLower)) ||  // J - name
                    (row[18] && String(row[18]).includes(searchQuery)) ||              // S - phone
                    (row[7] && String(row[7]).toLowerCase().includes(searchLower)) ||  // H - room number
                    (row[27] && String(row[27]).toLowerCase().includes(searchLower));  // AB - owner name
      
      if (match) {
        const rentalPeriods = parseRentalPeriods(row[25], row[26]);
        
        results.push({
          mobileCode: String(row[0] || ''),
          householdCode: String(row[1] || ''),
          province: String(row[2] || ''),
          district: String(row[3] || ''),
          village: String(row[4] || ''),
          unit: String(row[5] || ''),
          position: String(row[6] || ''),
          roomNumber: String(row[7] || ''),
          roomCount: String(row[8] || ''),
          name: String(row[9] || ''),
          birthDate: String(row[10] || ''),
          gender: String(row[11] || ''),
          nationality: String(row[12] || ''),
          ethnicity: String(row[13] || ''),
          religion: String(row[14] || ''),
          education: String(row[15] || ''),
          relationship: String(row[16] || ''),
          maritalStatus: String(row[17] || ''),
          phone: String(row[18] || ''),
          occupation: String(row[19] || ''),
          workplace: String(row[20] || ''),
          purpose: String(row[21] || ''),
          photo: String(row[22] || ''),
          hasPermit: String(row[23] || ''),
          firstArrival: String(row[24] || ''),
          rentalPeriods: rentalPeriods,
          ownerName: String(row[27] || ''),
          rentalType: row[1] ? 'ພັກເຊົາຄົງທີ່' : 'ພັກເຊົາເຄື່ອນທີ່'
        });
      }
    }
    
    Logger.log('Mobile search found: ' + results.length);
    return results;
    
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('ERROR in searchMobile: ' + error.message);
    return [];
  }
}

// ===================================
// Helper: Format Rental Periods
// ===================================
function formatRentalPeriods(periods) {
  if (!periods || periods.length === 0) {
    return '';
  }
  
  const parts = periods.map(function(p) {
    return 'ຄັ້ງທີ່ ' + p.periodNumber + ': ' + p.renewalDate + ' (' + p.days + ' ວັນ)';
  });
  
  return parts.join(' | ');
}

// ===================================
// Helper: Format End Dates
// ===================================
function formatEndDates(periods) {
  if (!periods || periods.length === 0) {
    return '';
  }
  
  const parts = periods.map(function(p) {
    return 'ຄັ້ງທີ່ ' + p.periodNumber + ': ' + p.endDate;
  });
  
  return parts.join(' | ');
}

// ===================================
// Calculate End Date
// ===================================
function calculateEndDate(startDate, days) {
  try {
    // Parse dd/mm/yyyy
    const parts = startDate.split('/');
    if (parts.length !== 3) {
      return '';
    }
    
    const day = parseInt(parts[0]);
    const month = parseInt(parts[1]) - 1; // JS months are 0-based
    const year = parseInt(parts[2]);
    
    const date = new Date(year, month, day);
    date.setDate(date.getDate() + parseInt(days));
    
    const endDay = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    const endMonth = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const endYear = date.getFullYear();
    
    return endDay + '/' + endMonth + '/' + endYear;
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('Error calculating end date: ' + error.message);
    return '';
  }
}


// ===================================
// DEBUG: ກວດສອບຂໍ້ມູນເບີໂທໃນຊີດ
// ===================================
function testPhoneCheck(inputPhone) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_IDS.LOGIN);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES.USER);
    const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
    const data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 9).getValues(); // ດຶງຖັນ A ຫາ I
    
    const fullPhone = '85620' + inputPhone;
    const results = [];
    
    data.forEach(function(row, idx) {
      const rawPhone = row[8] ? String(row[8]) : '';   // ຄ່າດິບໆ ຈາກຊີດ
      const digitsOnly = rawPhone.replace(/\D/g, '');    // ຕັດທຸກຢ່າງທີ່ບໍ່ແມ່ນຕົວເລກອອກ
      
      if (digitsOnly === fullPhone || rawPhone.trim() === fullPhone) {
        results.push({
          row: idx + 2,
          code: row[0],
          name: row[1],
          rawPhone: rawPhone,
          digitsOnly: digitsOnly
        });
      }
    });
    
    return {
      searchedFor: fullPhone,
      totalRows: data.length,
      matchCount: results.length,
      matches: results,
      samplePhones: data.slice(0, 5).map(function(r) { return String(r[8]); })
    };
  } catch (error) {
    return { error: error.message };
  }
}

// ===================================
// Update Position Only (ສຳລັບລາກ Marker ແກ້ໄຂຕໍາແໜ່ງ GPS)
// ===================================
function updateHouseholdPosition(householdCode, newPosition, villageSheetId) {
  try {
    if (!villageSheetId) {
      throw new Error('No village sheet ID');
    }
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(villageSheetId);
    const householdSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES.HOUSEHOLD);
    if (!householdSheet) {
      throw new Error('Household sheet not found');
    }

    const values = householdSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let i = 1; i < values.length; i++) {
      if (values[i][0] === householdCode) {
        householdSheet.getRange(i + 1, 16).setValue(newPosition); // ຖັນ P = housePosition
        return { success: true, message: 'ບັນທຶກຕໍາແໜ່ງໃໝ່ສຳເລັດ' };
      }
    }
    return { success: false, message: 'ບໍ່ພົບຄອບຄົວນີ້' };
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('ERROR in updateHouseholdPosition: ' + error.message);
    return { success: false, message: 'Error: ' + error.message };
  }
}

function updateMobilePosition(mobileCode, newPosition, villageSheetId) {
  try {
    if (!villageSheetId) {
      throw new Error('No village sheet ID');
    }
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(villageSheetId);
    const mobileSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAMES_MOBILE.MOBILE);
    if (!mobileSheet) {
      throw new Error('Mobile sheet not found');
    }

    const values = mobileSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let i = 1; i < values.length; i++) {
      if (values[i][0] === mobileCode) {
        mobileSheet.getRange(i + 1, 7).setValue(newPosition); // ຖັນ G = position
        return { success: true, message: 'ບັນທຶກຕໍາແໜ່ງໃໝ່ສຳເລັດ' };
      }
    }
    return { success: false, message: 'ບໍ່ພົບຂໍ້ມູນນີ້' };
  } catch (error) {
    Logger.log('ERROR in updateMobilePosition: ' + error.message);
    return { success: false, message: 'Error: ' + error.message };
  }
}
