      </div>
      <div class="form-group">
        <label>ອາຊີບ *</label>
        <input type="text" class="mobileMemberOccupation required-field" value="${member ? member.occupation || '' : ''}" list="occupationList" required data-field-name="ອາຊີບ">
      </div>
      <div class="form-group">
        <label>ລະບຸບ່ອນເຮັດວຽກ</label>
        <textarea class="mobileMemberWorkplace" rows="2" style="width: 100%; padding: 10px; border: 2px solid #e0e0e0; border-radius: 8px;">${member ? member.workplace || '' : ''}</textarea>
      </div>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Row 5: Purpose (Multi-Select Tags) -->
    <div class="form-row">
      <div class="form-group" style="grid-column: 1 / -1;">
        <label>ຈຸດປະສົງໃນການພັກເຊົ່າ *</label>
        <div class="disease-tags-container" id="purposeTagsContainer${counter}" style="display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 10px; min-height: 40px; padding: 10px; background: #f8f9fa; border: 2px dashed #dee2e6; border-radius: 8px;">
          <div style="width: 100%; text-align: center; color: #999; font-size: 13px;" class="empty-placeholder">
            ຍັງບໍ່ເລືອກຈຸດປະສົງ
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="position: relative;">
          <input type="text" 
                 class="mobilePurposeInput" 
                 id="purposeInput${counter}"
                 list="purposeList" 
                 placeholder="🔍 ເລືອກຫຼືພິມເອງ..."
                 style="width: 100%; padding: 12px 40px 12px 12px; border: 2px solid #e0e0e0; border-radius: 8px;"
                 onkeydown="handlePurposeInput(event, ${counter})">
          <button type="button" 
                  onclick="manualAddPurpose(${counter})"
                  style="position: absolute; right: 5px; top: 50%; transform: translateY(-50%); padding: 8px 15px; background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%); color: white; border: none; border-radius: 5px; cursor: pointer;">
            ➕ ເພີ່ມ
          </button>
        </div>
        <textarea class="mobileMemberPurpose required-field" 
                  id="purposeHidden${counter}"
                  rows="1" 
                  style="display: none;"
                  data-field-name="ຈຸດປະສົງ">${member ? member.purpose || '' : ''}</textarea>
      </div>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Row 6: Permit -->
    <div class="form-row">
      <div class="form-group">
        <label>ປື້ມອະນຸຍາດພັກເຊົ່າຊົ່ວຄາວ *</label>
        <div style="display: flex; gap: 10px;">
          <label style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 5px; cursor: pointer;">
            <input type="radio" name="mobilePermit${counter}" value="ມີ" onchange="togglePermitNumber(${counter}, true)" ${member && member.hasPermit === 'ມີ' ? 'checked' : ''}>
            <span>ມີ</span>
          </label>
          <label style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 5px; cursor: pointer;">
            <input type="radio" name="mobilePermit${counter}" value="ບໍ່ມີ" onchange="togglePermitNumber(${counter}, false)" ${!member || member.hasPermit !== 'ມີ' ? 'checked' : ''}>
            <span>ບໍ່ມີ</span>
          </label>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="form-group" id="permitNumberGroup${counter}" style="display: ${member && member.hasPermit === 'ມີ' ? 'block' : 'none'};">
        <label>ເລກທີປື້ມ</label>
        <input type="text" class="mobilePermitNumber" id="permitNumber${counter}" value="${member ? member.permitNumber || '' : ''}">
      </div>
      <div class="form-group">
        <label>ວັນທີມາພັກເຊົ່າຄັ້ງທໍາອິດ</label>
        <input type="date" class="mobileFirstArrival" value="${member ? member.firstArrival || '' : ''}">
      </div>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Row 7: Rental Periods (Dynamic) -->
    <div class="form-row">
      <div class="form-group" style="grid-column: 1 / -1;">
        <label>ວັນທີອອກການພັກເຊົ່າ & ວັນທີສິ້ນສຸດ</label>
        <div class="rental-periods-container" id="rentalPeriodsContainer${counter}">
          <div id="rentalPeriodsList${counter}">
            <!-- Rental periods will be added here -->
          </div>
          <button type="button" class="btn-add-period" onclick="addRentalPeriod(${counter})">
            ➕ ເພີ່ມການຕໍ່ອາຍຸ
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

// ===================================
// Remove Mobile Member Form
// ===================================
function removeMobileMemberForm(id) {
  const memberDiv = document.getElementById(`mobile-member-${id}`);
  if (memberDiv && confirm('ທ່ານຕ້ອງການລົບສະມາຊິກຄົນນີ້ບໍ່?')) {
    memberDiv.remove();
    reorderMobileMembers();
    updateMobileRoomCount();
  }
}

function reorderMobileMembers() {
  const container = document.getElementById('mobileMembersContainer');
  const memberDivs = container.querySelectorAll('.mobile-member-section');
  
  memberDivs.forEach(function(div, index) {
    const newNumber = index + 1;
    const heading = div.querySelector('h3');
    if (heading) {
      heading.setAttribute('data-member-number', newNumber);
      const span = heading.querySelector('span');
      if (span) {
        span.textContent = '👤 ສະມາຊິກຄົນທີ່ ' + newNumber;
      }
    }
    div.id = 'mobile-member-' + newNumber;
  });
  
  mobileMemberCounter = memberDivs.length;
}

// ===================================
// Update Mobile Room Count
// ===================================
function updateMobileRoomCount() {
  const memberForms = document.querySelectorAll('#mobileMembersContainer .mobile-member-section');
  const totalMembers = memberForms.length;
  
  const roomCountField = document.getElementById('mobileRoomCount');
  if (roomCountField) {
    roomCountField.value = totalMembers;
  }
}

// ===================================
// Toggle Permit Number Field
// ===================================
function togglePermitNumber(memberId, show) {
  const permitNumberGroup = document.getElementById('permitNumberGroup' + memberId);
  const permitNumberInput = document.getElementById('permitNumber' + memberId);
  
  if (permitNumberGroup) {
    permitNumberGroup.style.display = show ? 'block' : 'none';
  }
  
  if (permitNumberInput && !show) {
    permitNumberInput.value = '';
  }
}

// ===================================
// Rental Periods Management
// ===================================
var rentalPeriodCounters = {};

function addRentalPeriod(memberId) {
  if (!rentalPeriodCounters[memberId]) {
    rentalPeriodCounters[memberId] = 0;
  }
  rentalPeriodCounters[memberId]++;
  
  const periodId = rentalPeriodCounters[memberId];
  const container = document.getElementById('rentalPeriodsList' + memberId);
  
  const periodDiv = document.createElement('div');
  periodDiv.className = 'rental-period-item';
  periodDiv.id = 'rentalPeriod' + memberId + '-' + periodId;
  periodDiv.innerHTML = '\
    <label>ຄັ້ງທີ່ ' + periodId + ':</label>\
    <input type="date" \
           class="date-input rentalStartDate" \
           id="rentalStart' + memberId + '-' + periodId + '"\
           placeholder="ວັນທີອອກການ"\
           onchange="calculateEndDate(' + memberId + ', ' + periodId + ')">\
    <input type="number" \
           class="days-input rentalDays" \
           id="rentalDays' + memberId + '-' + periodId + '"\
           placeholder="ຈໍານວນວັນ"\
           min="1"\
           onchange="calculateEndDate(' + memberId + ', ' + periodId + ')">\
    <span>ວັນ</span>\
    <div class="end-date-display" id="rentalEnd' + memberId + '-' + periodId + '">\
      -\
    </div>\
    <button type="button" class="btn-remove-period" onclick="removeRentalPeriod(' + memberId + ', ' + periodId + ')">\
      ✖\
    </button>\
  ';
  
  container.appendChild(periodDiv);
}

function removeRentalPeriod(memberId, periodId) {
  const periodDiv = document.getElementById('rentalPeriod' + memberId + '-' + periodId);
  if (periodDiv && confirm('ລົບການຕໍ່ອາຍຸນີ້ບໍ່?')) {
    periodDiv.remove();
  }
}

function calculateEndDate(memberId, periodId) {
  const startDateInput = document.getElementById('rentalStart' + memberId + '-' + periodId);
  const daysInput = document.getElementById('rentalDays' + memberId + '-' + periodId);
  const endDateDisplay = document.getElementById('rentalEnd' + memberId + '-' + periodId);
  
  if (!startDateInput || !daysInput || !endDateDisplay) return;
  
  const startDate = startDateInput.value;
  const days = parseInt(daysInput.value);
  
  if (startDate && days > 0) {
    const start = new Date(startDate);
    const end = new Date(start);
    end.setDate(start.getDate() + days);
    
    const endDateStr = end.toISOString().split('T')[0];
    endDateDisplay.textContent = 'ສິ້ນສຸດ: ' + endDateStr;
    endDateDisplay.setAttribute('data-end-date', endDateStr);
  } else {
    endDateDisplay.textContent = '-';
    endDateDisplay.removeAttribute('data-end-date');
  }
}

// ===================================
// Purpose Tags Management
// ===================================
function handlePurposeInput(event, memberId) {
  const input = document.getElementById('purposeInput' + memberId);
  const value = input.value.trim();
  
  if (event.key === 'Enter') {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (value) {
      addPurposeTag(value, memberId);
      input.value = '';
    }
  }
}

function manualAddPurpose(memberId) {
  const input = document.getElementById('purposeInput' + memberId);
  const value = input.value.trim();
  
  if (value) {
    addPurposeTag(value, memberId);
    input.value = '';
    input.focus();
  } else {
    showToast('ກະລຸນາພິມຈຸດປະສົງກ່ອນ', 'error');
    input.focus();
  }
}

function addPurposeTag(purpose, memberId) {
  const container = document.getElementById('purposeTagsContainer' + memberId);
  const hiddenTextarea = document.getElementById('purposeHidden' + memberId);
  
  if (!container || !hiddenTextarea) return;
  
  const placeholder = container.querySelector('.empty-placeholder');
  if (placeholder) {
    placeholder.remove();
  }
  
  const existingTags = container.querySelectorAll('.disease-tag');
  for (var i = 0; i < existingTags.length; i++) {
    if (existingTags[i].getAttribute('data-purpose') === purpose) {
      showToast('⚠️ ມີຈຸດປະສົງນີ້ແລ້ວ', 'error');
      return;
    }
  }
  
  const tag = document.createElement('span');
  tag.className = 'disease-tag';
  tag.setAttribute('data-purpose', purpose);
  tag.style.cssText = 'background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fff3e0 0%, #ffe0b2 100%); color: #f57c00; padding: 8px 12px; border-radius: 20px; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; font-size: 0.9rem; box-shadow: 0 2px 5px rgba(0,0,0,0.1);';
  tag.innerHTML = '\
    <span style="font-weight: 500;">📌 ' + purpose + '</span>\
    <span onclick="removePurposeTag(this, ' + memberId + ')" \
          style="cursor: pointer; font-weight: bold; font-size: 1.2rem; color: #f44336;">×</span>\
  ';
  
  container.appendChild(tag);
  updatePurposeTextarea(memberId);
  showToast('✅ ເພີ່ມຈຸດປະສົງສຳເລັດ', 'success');
}

function removePurposeTag(element, memberId) {
  const tag = element.parentElement;
  tag.remove();
  updatePurposeTextarea(memberId);
}

function updatePurposeTextarea(memberId) {
  const container = document.getElementById('purposeTagsContainer' + memberId);
  const hiddenTextarea = document.getElementById('purposeHidden' + memberId);
  
  if (!container || !hiddenTextarea) return;
  
  const tags = container.querySelectorAll('.disease-tag');
  const purposes = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < tags.length; i++) {
    purposes.push(tags[i].getAttribute('data-purpose'));
  }
  
  hiddenTextarea.value = purposes.join(', ');
}
// ===================================
// NEW MULTI-STEP LOGIN FLOW
// ===================================
const PHONE_PREFIX = '85620';
let loginFlowSelectedUser = null;

function goToLoginStep(n) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.login-step').forEach(el => el.style.display = 'none');
  document.getElementById('loginStep' + n).style.display = 'block';
}

function backLoginStep(n) {
  goToLoginStep(n);
}

function verifyPhoneStep() {
  const phoneInput = document.getElementById('loginPhone').value.trim();

  if (!/^\d{8}$/.test(phoneInput)) {
    showToast('ກະລຸນາປ້ອນເບີໂທ 8 ຕົວເລກ', 'error');
    return;
  }

  if (!loginData || !loginData.users) {
    showToast('ຂໍ້ມູນຍັງບໍ່ພ້ອມ ກະລຸນາລໍຖ້າ', 'error');
    return;
  }

  // ✅ FIX: เทียบเฉพาะเลข 8 หลักสุดท้ายของเบอร์โทร
  // ไม่ว่าในชีตจะเก็บแบบมีรหัสประเทศ 856, มีเลข 0 นำหน้า, มีขีด/เว้นวรรค ก็แมตช์ได้หมด
  const matches = loginData.users.filter(u => {
    const digitsOnly = (u.phone || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
    return digitsOnly.slice(-8) === phoneInput;
  });

  if (matches.length === 0) {
    showToast('ບໍ່ພົບເບີໂທນີ້ໃນລະບົບ', 'error');
    return;
  }

  goToLoginStep(2);
}

function verifyLocationStep() {
  const province = document.getElementById('loginProvinceValue').value.trim();
  const district = document.getElementById('loginDistrictValue').value.trim();
  const village = document.getElementById('loginVillageValue').value.trim();

  if (!province || !district || !village) {
    showToast('ກະລຸນາເລືອກແຂວງ, ເມືອງ, ບ້ານ ຈາກລາຍການໃຫ້ຄົບ (ຕ້ອງກົດເລືອກ, ພິມແລ້ວບໍ່ກົດຈະບໍ່ນັບ)', 'error');
    return;
  }

  const namesInVillage = loginData.users.filter(u =>
    u.province === province && u.district === district && u.village === village
  );

  if (namesInVillage.length === 0) {
    showToast('ບໍ່ພົບຂໍ້ມູນ ອສບ ໃນບ້ານນີ້', 'error');
    return;
  }

  renderNameSelectionStep(namesInVillage);
  goToLoginStep(3);
}

function renderNameSelectionStep(users) {
  const container = document.getElementById('loginNameListContainer');
  container.innerHTML = '';

  users.forEach(user => {
    const card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'household-card';
    card.innerHTML = '<div class="household-card-info"><h4>' + user.name + '</h4></div>';
    card.onclick = function() { selectLoginName(user, card); };
    container.appendChild(card);
  });
}

function selectLoginName(user, cardEl) {
  document.querySelectorAll('#loginNameListContainer .household-card').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('selected'));
  cardEl.classList.add('selected');
  loginFlowSelectedUser = user;
  setTimeout(() => goToLoginStep(4), 200);
}

function finalLoginSubmit() {
  const password = document.getElementById('loginPassword').value.trim();

  if (!loginFlowSelectedUser) {
    showToast('ກະລຸນາເລືອກຊື່ກ່ອນ', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if (!password) {
    showToast('ກະລຸນາປ້ອນລະຫັດຜ່ານ', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if (password !== loginFlowSelectedUser.code) {
    showToast('ລະຫັດຜ່ານບໍ່ຖືກຕ້ອງ', 'error');
    return;
  }

  const loginInfo = {
    code: loginFlowSelectedUser.code,
    name: loginFlowSelectedUser.name,
    province: loginFlowSelectedUser.province,
    district: loginFlowSelectedUser.district,
    village: loginFlowSelectedUser.village
  };

showLoading();
  google.script.run
    .withSuccessHandler(function(result) {
      hideLoading();
      if (result.success) {
        villageSheetId = result.villageSheetId;
        currentUser = result;
        saveLoginSession(); // ✅ ບັນທຶກ session ໄວ້ 24 ຊົ່ວໂມງ
        showMainApp();
        showToast('ເຂົ້າສູ່ລະບົບສຳເລັດ', 'success');
      } else {
        showToast(result.message, 'error');
      }
    })
    .withFailureHandler(function(error) {
      hideLoading();
      showToast('ເກີດຂໍ້ຜິດພາດ: ' + error.message, 'error');
    })
    .validateLogin(loginInfo);
}

const HOUSEHOLD_TEXT_FIELDS = ['householdCode','name','photo','phone','registryNumber','housePosition','rentalLocation','houseNumber'];
const MEMBER_TEXT_FIELDS = ['householdCode','memberCode','name','photo','phone','housePosition','birthDate'];

function getHouseholdFilterColumns() { return HOUSEHOLD_COLUMNS; }
function getMemberFilterColumns() { return MEMBER_COLUMNS; }

let summaryFilters = { household: {}, member: {} };
let hiddenColumns = { household: new Set(), member: new Set() };
let currentFilterScope = 'both';
let summaryDataType = 'household';
let summaryActiveTab = 'table';
let gpsSummaryMap = null;
let summaryChartInstances = [];
let ageChartInstance = null;

let gpsMarkersData = {};
let currentEditingMarker = null;
let myLocationMarker = null;

let gpsSelectedUnits = [];

function getRelationshipRank(rel) {
  const cleanRel = (rel || '').toString().trim().normalize('NFC');
  if (!cleanRel) return 999;

  // 1) ຫົວໜ້າຄອບຄົວ ต้องตรงเป๊ะทั้งข้อความเท่านั้น (ไม่ใช่แค่มีคำนี้ปนอยู่ในคำอื่น)
  if (cleanRel === 'ຫົວໜ້າຄອບຄົວ') return 0;

  // 2) ຜົວ/ເມຍ ຫົວໜ້າຄອບຄົວ (เช่น "ເມຍຫົວໜ້າຄອບຄົວ", "ຜົວຫົວໜ້າຄອບຄົວ")
  const spouseKeywords = ['ເມຍ', 'ຜົວ', 'ພັນລະຍາ', 'ສາມີ'];
  if (spouseKeywords.some(function(k) { return cleanRel.indexOf(k) !== -1; })) return 1;

  // 3) ລູກເຂີຍ / ລູກໃພ້ (ต้องเช็คก่อน "ลูก" เฉยๆ เพราะมีคำว่า "ลูก" ปนอยู่ข้างใน)
  const childInLawKeywords = ['ລູກເຂີຍ', 'ລູກໃພ້'];
  if (childInLawKeywords.some(function(k) { return cleanRel.indexOf(k) !== -1; })) return 3;

  // 4) ລູກ
  if (cleanRel.indexOf('ລູກ') !== -1) return 2;

  // 5) ຫຼານ / ຫລານ
  if (cleanRel.indexOf('ຫຼານ') !== -1 || cleanRel.indexOf('ຫລານ') !== -1) return 4;

  // 6) ພໍ່ / ແມ່
  if (cleanRel.indexOf('ພໍ່') !== -1 || cleanRel.indexOf('ແມ່') !== -1) return 5;

  // 7) ພີ່ນ້ອງ
  if (cleanRel.indexOf('ພີ່ນ້ອງ') !== -1) return 6;

  // นอกเหนือจากนี้ ให้อยู่ท้ายสุด
  return 999;
}

function sortMembersByRelationship(members) {
  return [...members].sort((a, b) => {
    const rankA = getRelationshipRank(a.relationship);
    const rankB = getRelationshipRank(b.relationship);
    if (rankA !== rankB) return rankA - rankB;
    return (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || '', 'lo');
  });
}

function calculateAgeFromBirthDate(birthDateStr) {
  if (!birthDateStr) return null;
  const parts = birthDateStr.split('/');
  if (parts.length !== 3) return null;
  const day = parseInt(parts[0]), month = parseInt(parts[1]), year = parseInt(parts[2]);
  if (!day || !month || !year || isNaN(day) || isNaN(month) || isNaN(year)) return null;
  const birth = new Date(year, month - 1, day);
  const today = new Date();
  let age = today.getFullYear() - birth.getFullYear();
  const m = today.getMonth() - birth.getMonth();
  if (m < 0 || (m === 0 && today.getDate() < birth.getDate())) age--;
  return age >= 0 ? age : null;
}
function calculateAgeFromBirthDate(birthDateStr) {
  if (!birthDateStr) return null;
  const parts = birthDateStr.split('/');
  if (parts.length !== 3) return null;
  const day = parseInt(parts[0]), month = parseInt(parts[1]), year = parseInt(parts[2]);
  if (!day || !month || !year || isNaN(day) || isNaN(month) || isNaN(year)) return null;
  const birth = new Date(year, month - 1, day);
  const today = new Date();
  let age = today.getFullYear() - birth.getFullYear();
  const m = today.getMonth() - birth.getMonth();
  if (m < 0 || (m === 0 && today.getDate() < birth.getDate())) age--;
  return age >= 0 ? age : null;
}

function getActiveAgeRanges() {
  const ranges = summaryFilters.member && summaryFilters.member.age;
  if (!ranges || !Array.isArray(ranges) || ranges.length === 0) return [];
  return ranges.filter(function(r) { return r.min !== '' || r.max !== ''; });
}

function formatAgeRangeLabel(range) {
  if (range.min !== '' && range.max !== '') return range.min + '-' + range.max + ' ปี';
  if (range.min !== '' && range.max === '') return range.min + '+ ปี';
  if (range.min === '' && range.max !== '') return '≤' + range.max + ' ปี';
  return 'ทุกช่วงอายุ';
}

function isAgeInRange(age, range) {
  if (age === null) return false;
  if (range.min !== '' && age < parseInt(range.min)) return false;
  if (range.max !== '' && age > parseInt(range.max)) return false;
  return true;
}
function fieldPassesFilter(item, colKey, filterState, rangeFields, multiFields, textFields) {
  const filterVal = filterState[colKey];
  if (filterVal === undefined || filterVal === null) return true;

if (colKey === 'age') {
    // ✅ อายุใช้แค่ "สร้างคอลัมน์ + ติ๊กถูก" ในตาราง ไม่ใช้กรองสมาชิกออกจากรายชื่อ
    return true;
  }

  if (rangeFields.indexOf(colKey) !== -1) {
    if (!filterVal || (filterVal.min === '' && filterVal.max === '')) return true;
    const num = parseFloat(item[colKey]);
    if (filterVal.min !== '' && (isNaN(num) || num < parseFloat(filterVal.min))) return false;
    if (filterVal.max !== '' && (isNaN(num) || num > parseFloat(filterVal.max))) return false;
    return true;
  }

if (multiFields.indexOf(colKey) !== -1) {
    if (!filterVal || filterVal.length === 0) return true;
    if (colKey === 'disease') {
      const itemDisease = item.disease || '';
      return filterVal.some(function(token) { return itemDisease.indexOf(token) !== -1; });
    }
    return filterVal.indexOf(item[colKey]) !== -1;
  }

  if (textFields && textFields.indexOf(colKey) !== -1) {
    if (filterVal === '') return true;
    const itemText = String(item[colKey] || '').toLowerCase();
    return itemText.indexOf(String(filterVal).toLowerCase()) !== -1;
  }

  if (filterVal === '') return true;
  return String(item[colKey] || '') === filterVal;
}

function householdPassesFilters(household) {
  const columns = getHouseholdFilterColumns();
  const filterState = summaryFilters.household;
  return columns.every(function(col) {
    return fieldPassesFilter(household, col.key, filterState, HOUSEHOLD_RANGE_FIELDS, HOUSEHOLD_MULTISELECT_FIELDS, HOUSEHOLD_TEXT_FIELDS);
  });
}

function memberPassesFilters(member) {
  const columns = getMemberFilterColumns();
  const filterState = summaryFilters.member;
  return columns.every(function(col) {
    return fieldPassesFilter(member, col.key, filterState, MEMBER_RANGE_FIELDS, MEMBER_MULTISELECT_FIELDS, MEMBER_TEXT_FIELDS);
  });
}

let summaryModalOpening = false;

function openSummaryModal(type) {
  if (summaryModalOpening) return;
  summaryModalOpening = true;

  const btn = document.getElementById('btnOpenSummary');
  if (btn) {
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.style.opacity = '0.6';
    btn.style.cursor = 'not-allowed';
  }

  showLoading(0, 'ກຳລັງກະກຽມຂໍ້ມູນສະຫຼຸບ...');

  setTimeout(function() {
    summaryDataType = type;
    summaryActiveTab = 'table';

    document.getElementById('summaryModalTitle').textContent =
      type === 'household' ? '📊 ສະຫຼຸບຂໍ້ມູນປະຊາກອນຄົງທີ່' : '📊 ສະຫຼຸບຂໍ້ມູນປະຊາກອນເຄື່ອນທີ່';

    updateActiveFilterCount();
    switchSummaryTab('table');

    document.getElementById('summaryModal').classList.add('active');
    hideLoading();

    if (btn) {
      btn.disabled = false;
      btn.style.opacity = '1';
      btn.style.cursor = 'pointer';
    }
    summaryModalOpening = false;
  }, 50);
}

function closeSummaryModal() {
  document.getElementById('summaryModal').classList.remove('active');
}
function getUniqueValuesForColumn(dataArray, key) {
  const set = new Set();
  dataArray.forEach(function(item) {
    const val = item[key];
    if (val !== undefined && val !== null && String(val).trim() !== '') {
      set.add(String(val).trim());
    }
  });
  const values = Array.from(set);
  if (key === 'unit') {
    return values.sort(naturalUnitCompare);
  }
  return values.sort(function(a,b){ return a.localeCompare(b, 'lo'); });
}

function getUniqueDiseaseTokens(members) {
  const set = new Set();
  members.forEach(function(m) {
    if (!m.disease) return;
    m.disease.split(',').forEach(function(d) {
      const t = d.trim();
      if (t) set.add(t);
    });
  });
  return Array.from(set).sort(function(a,b){ return a.localeCompare(b,'lo'); });
}

function getAllMembersFlat() {
  let all = [];
  householdsData.forEach(function(h) { all = all.concat(h.members || []); });
  return all;
}

}

function selectFilterScope(scope) {

  closeFilterScopeModal();
