}

function openFilterFieldsModal(scope) {
  const titles = {
    household: '🏠 ຕົວກອງຫົວໜ້າຄອບຄົວ (A-T)',
    member: '👥 ຕົວກອງສະມາຊິກຄອບຄົວ (A-W)',
    both: '🏠+👥 ຕົວກອງຫົວໜ້າຄອບຄົວ ແລະ ສະມາຊິກ'
  };
  document.getElementById('filterFieldsModalTitle').textContent = titles[scope] || '🔍 ຕົວກອງ';

  const body = document.getElementById('filterFieldsBody');
  body.innerHTML = '';

  if (scope === 'both') {
    const wrap = document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className = 'filter-two-col';

    const leftCol = document.createElement('div');
    leftCol.className = 'filter-column-group';
    leftCol.innerHTML = '<h4>🏠 ຫົວໜ້າຄອບຄົວ (A-T)</h4>';
    leftCol.appendChild(buildFilterFieldsContainer('household'));

    const rightCol = document.createElement('div');
    rightCol.className = 'filter-column-group';
    rightCol.innerHTML = '<h4>👥 ສະມາຊິກຄອບຄົວ (A-W)</h4>';
    rightCol.appendChild(buildFilterFieldsContainer('member'));

    wrap.appendChild(leftCol);
    wrap.appendChild(rightCol);
    body.appendChild(wrap);
  } else {
    body.appendChild(buildFilterFieldsContainer(scope));
  }

  document.getElementById('filterFieldsModal').classList.add('active');
}

function closeFilterFieldsModal() {
  document.getElementById('filterFieldsModal').classList.remove('active');
}

function buildFilterFieldsContainer(scope) {
  const container = document.createElement('div');

const columns = scope === 'household' ? getHouseholdFilterColumns() : getMemberFilterColumns();
  const rangeFields = scope === 'household' ? HOUSEHOLD_RANGE_FIELDS : MEMBER_RANGE_FIELDS;
  const multiFields = scope === 'household' ? HOUSEHOLD_MULTISELECT_FIELDS : MEMBER_MULTISELECT_FIELDS;
  const textFields = scope === 'household' ? HOUSEHOLD_TEXT_FIELDS : MEMBER_TEXT_FIELDS;
  const dataArray = scope === 'household' ? householdsData : getAllMembersFlat();
  const filterState = summaryFilters[scope];
  const hiddenSet = hiddenColumns[scope];

  columns.forEach(function(col) {
  const row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'filter-field-row';

    const labelRow = document.createElement('div');
    labelRow.className = 'filter-field-label-row';
    const isHidden = hiddenSet.has(col.key);
    if (isHidden) {
      row.classList.add('hidden-column-active');
    }
    labelRow.innerHTML =
      '<label>' + col.label + '</label>' +
      '<label class="filter-hide-toggle">' +
        '<input type="checkbox" data-hide-scope="' + scope + '" data-hide-key="' + col.key + '" ' + (isHidden ? 'checked' : '') + ' onchange="onHideColumnToggle(this)"> ເຊື່ອງຫົວຂໍ້ນີ້' +
      '</label>';
    row.appendChild(labelRow);

  if (col.key === 'age') {
      const currentRanges = (filterState.age && Array.isArray(filterState.age) && filterState.age.length > 0)
        ? filterState.age
        : [{ min: '', max: '' }];

      const ageRangesDiv = document.createElement('div');
      ageRangesDiv.className = 'age-ranges-list';
      ageRangesDiv.id = 'ageRangesList_' + scope;
      ageRangesDiv.setAttribute('data-scope', scope);

      currentRanges.forEach(function(range) {
        ageRangesDiv.appendChild(buildAgeRangeRow(scope, range));
      });
      row.appendChild(ageRangesDiv);

      const addAgeBtn = document.createElement('button');
      addAgeBtn.type = 'button';
      addAgeBtn.textContent = '➕ ເພີ່ມຊ່ວງອາຍຸ';
      addAgeBtn.style.cssText = 'margin-top:8px; padding:8px 15px; background:linear-gradient(135deg,#4caf50 0%,#66bb6a 100%); color:white; border:none; border-radius:6px; cursor:pointer; font-size:13px; font-weight:600;';
      addAgeBtn.onclick = function() { addAgeRangeRow(scope); };
      row.appendChild(addAgeBtn);
    } else if (rangeFields.indexOf(col.key) !== -1) {
      const current = filterState[col.key] || { min: '', max: '' };
      const rangeDiv = document.createElement('div');
      rangeDiv.className = 'filter-range-inputs';
      rangeDiv.innerHTML =
        '<input type="number" placeholder="ຕໍ່າສຸດ" value="' + (current.min || '') + '" data-scope="' + scope + '" data-key="' + col.key + '" data-range="min">' +
        '<span>ถึง</span>' +
        '<input type="number" placeholder="ສູງສຸດ" value="' + (current.max || '') + '" data-scope="' + scope + '" data-key="' + col.key + '" data-range="max">';
      row.appendChild(rangeDiv);
} else if (multiFields.indexOf(col.key) !== -1) {
      const uniqueVals = col.key === 'disease' ? getUniqueDiseaseTokens(dataArray) : getUniqueValuesForColumn(dataArray, col.key);
      const currentSelected = filterState[col.key] || [];
      const checkboxWrap = document.createElement('div');
      checkboxWrap.className = 'filter-checkbox-list';
      checkboxWrap.setAttribute('data-scope', scope);
      checkboxWrap.setAttribute('data-key', col.key);
      checkboxWrap.setAttribute('data-multiselect', '1');
      checkboxWrap.style.cssText = 'max-height:180px; overflow-y:auto; border:2px solid #e0e0e0; border-radius:8px; padding:8px; background:white;';
      if (uniqueVals.length === 0) {
        checkboxWrap.innerHTML = '<div style="color:#999; text-align:center; padding:5px;">ບໍ່ມີຂໍ້ມູນ</div>';
      } else {
        uniqueVals.forEach(function(v) {
          const label = document.createElement('label');
          label.style.cssText = 'display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; padding:6px 4px; cursor:pointer; border-bottom:1px solid #f5f5f5;';
          const checked = currentSelected.indexOf(v) !== -1 ? 'checked' : '';
          label.innerHTML = '<input type="checkbox" value="' + v + '" ' + checked + ' style="width:18px; height:18px; cursor:pointer;"> <span>' + v + '</span>';
          checkboxWrap.appendChild(label);
        });
      }
     row.appendChild(checkboxWrap);
    } else if (textFields.indexOf(col.key) !== -1) {
      const currentTextVal = filterState[col.key] || '';
      const textInput = document.createElement('input');
      textInput.type = 'text';
      textInput.placeholder = 'พิมพ์เพื่อค้นหา ' + col.label + '...';
      textInput.value = currentTextVal;
      textInput.setAttribute('data-scope', scope);
      textInput.setAttribute('data-key', col.key);
      textInput.setAttribute('data-textfilter', '1');
      textInput.style.cssText = 'width:100%; padding:8px 10px; border:2px solid #e0e0e0; border-radius:6px; font-size:14px;';
      row.appendChild(textInput);
    } else {
      const uniqueVals = getUniqueValuesForColumn(dataArray, col.key);
      const select = document.createElement('select');
      select.setAttribute('data-scope', scope);
      select.setAttribute('data-key', col.key);
      const allOpt = document.createElement('option');
      allOpt.value = '';
      allOpt.textContent = 'ทั้งหมด';
      select.appendChild(allOpt);
      uniqueVals.forEach(function(v) {
        const opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = v;
        opt.textContent = v;
        if (filterState[col.key] === v) opt.selected = true;
        select.appendChild(opt);
      });
      row.appendChild(select);
    }

    container.appendChild(row);
  });

  return container;
}
function buildAgeRangeRow(scope, range) {
  const div = document.createElement('div');
  div.className = 'filter-range-inputs age-range-row';
  div.style.marginBottom = '8px';
  div.innerHTML =
    '<input type="number" placeholder="ຈາກອາຍຸ" min="0" value="' + (range.min || '') + '" data-scope="' + scope + '" data-age-range="min">' +
    '<span>ເຖິງ</span>' +
    '<input type="number" placeholder="ເຖິງອາຍຸ" min="0" value="' + (range.max || '') + '" data-scope="' + scope + '" data-age-range="max">' +
    '<button type="button" onclick="this.parentElement.remove()" style="flex-shrink:0; padding:8px 12px; background:#f44336; color:white; border:none; border-radius:5px; cursor:pointer;">✖</button>';
  return div;
}

function addAgeRangeRow(scope) {
  const list = document.getElementById('ageRangesList_' + scope);
  if (!list) return;
  list.appendChild(buildAgeRangeRow(scope, { min: '', max: '' }));
}
function onHideColumnToggle(checkbox) {
  const scope = checkbox.getAttribute('data-hide-scope');
  const key = checkbox.getAttribute('data-hide-key');
  const row = checkbox.closest('.filter-field-row');
  if (checkbox.checked) {
    hiddenColumns[scope].add(key);
    if (row) row.classList.add('hidden-column-active');
  } else {
    hiddenColumns[scope].delete(key);
    if (row) row.classList.remove('hidden-column-active');
  }
}
function applyNewFilters() {
  const body = document.getElementById('filterFieldsBody');

  body.querySelectorAll('[data-multiselect="1"]').forEach(function(wrap) {
    const scope = wrap.getAttribute('data-scope');
    const key = wrap.getAttribute('data-key');
    const checkedBoxes = wrap.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]:checked');
    const values = Array.from(checkedBoxes).map(function(cb) { return cb.value; });
    if (values.length === 0) {
      delete summaryFilters[scope][key];
    } else {
      summaryFilters[scope][key] = values;
    }
  });

body.querySelectorAll('select:not([data-multiselect="1"])').forEach(function(select) {
    const scope = select.getAttribute('data-scope');
    const key = select.getAttribute('data-key');
    if (select.value === '') {
      delete summaryFilters[scope][key];
    } else {
      summaryFilters[scope][key] = select.value;
    }
  });

  body.querySelectorAll('input[data-textfilter="1"]').forEach(function(input) {
    const scope = input.getAttribute('data-scope');
    const key = input.getAttribute('data-key');
    const val = input.value.trim();
    if (val === '') {
      delete summaryFilters[scope][key];
    } else {
      summaryFilters[scope][key] = val;
    }
  });

body.querySelectorAll('input[data-range]').forEach(function(input) {
    const scope = input.getAttribute('data-scope');
    const key = input.getAttribute('data-key');
    const rangeType = input.getAttribute('data-range');
    if (!summaryFilters[scope][key] || typeof summaryFilters[scope][key] !== 'object' || Array.isArray(summaryFilters[scope][key])) {
      summaryFilters[scope][key] = { min: '', max: '' };
    }
    summaryFilters[scope][key][rangeType] = input.value;
    if (summaryFilters[scope][key].min === '' && summaryFilters[scope][key].max === '') {
      delete summaryFilters[scope][key];
    }
  });

  body.querySelectorAll('.age-ranges-list').forEach(function(listEl) {
    const scope = listEl.getAttribute('data-scope');
    const rows = listEl.querySelectorAll('.age-range-row');
    const ranges = [];
    rows.forEach(function(rowEl) {
      const minInput = rowEl.querySelector('input[data-age-range="min"]');
      const maxInput = rowEl.querySelector('input[data-age-range="max"]');
      const min = minInput ? minInput.value : '';
      const max = maxInput ? maxInput.value : '';
      if (min !== '' || max !== '') {
        ranges.push({ min: min, max: max });
      }
    });
    if (ranges.length === 0) {
      delete summaryFilters[scope].age;
    } else {
      summaryFilters[scope].age = ranges;
    }
  });

  updateActiveFilterCount();
  closeFilterFieldsModal();
  renderSummaryContent();
  showToast('นำใช้ตัวกรองสำเร็จ', 'success');
}

function resetAllFilters() {
  summaryFilters = { household: {}, member: {} };
  hiddenColumns = { household: new Set(), member: new Set() };
  updateActiveFilterCount();
  renderSummaryContent();
  showToast('ล้างตัวกรองทั้งหมดสำเร็จ', 'success');
}

function updateActiveFilterCount() {
  let count = 0;
  ['household','member'].forEach(function(scope) {
    Object.keys(summaryFilters[scope]).forEach(function(key) {
      const val = summaryFilters[scope][key];
      if (Array.isArray(val)) {
        if (val.length > 0) count++;
      } else if (val && typeof val === 'object') {
        if (val.min !== '' || val.max !== '') count++;
      } else if (val && val !== '') {
        count++;
      }
    });
  });
  const badge = document.getElementById('activeFilterCount');
  if (badge) badge.textContent = count;
}
function getFilteredHouseholdSummary() {
  const result = [];

  // ✅ "หน่วย" ถือเป็นคุณสมบัติระดับครอบครัวเสมอ ไม่ว่าจะไปติ๊กเลือกจากฝั่งหัวหน้าครอบครัว
  // หรือฝั่งสมาชิกก็ตาม เพื่อกันปัญหาสมาชิกหายเวลากรองด้วยหน่วย
  const effectiveUnitFilter =
    (summaryFilters.household.unit && summaryFilters.household.unit.length > 0) ? summaryFilters.household.unit :
    (summaryFilters.member.unit && summaryFilters.member.unit.length > 0) ? summaryFilters.member.unit :
    null;

  const householdFilterKeys = Object.keys(summaryFilters.household).filter(function(k){ return k !== 'unit'; });
  const memberFilterKeys = Object.keys(summaryFilters.member).filter(function(k){ return k !== 'unit'; });

  const householdFilterActive = householdFilterKeys.length > 0;
  const memberFilterActive = memberFilterKeys.length > 0;

  const applyHouseholdFilter = (currentFilterScope === 'household' || currentFilterScope === 'both') && householdFilterActive;
  const applyMemberFilter = (currentFilterScope === 'member' || currentFilterScope === 'both') && memberFilterActive;

  householdsData.forEach(function(household) {
    // ✅ กรอง "หน่วย" ที่ระดับครอบครัวก่อนเสมอ
    if (effectiveUnitFilter && effectiveUnitFilter.indexOf(household.unit) === -1) return;

    if (applyHouseholdFilter && !householdPassesFilters(household)) return;

    let matchingMembers = household.members || [];
    if (applyMemberFilter) {
      matchingMembers = matchingMembers.filter(memberPassesFiltersExcludingUnit);
    }

    result.push({
      household: household,
      members: sortMembersByRelationship(matchingMembers)
    });
  });
  return result;
}

// ✅ ฟังก์ชันใหม่: ตรวจสอบสมาชิกตามตัวกรอง แต่ไม่รวม "หน่วย" (เพราะกรองที่ระดับครอบครัวไปแล้วด้านบน)
function memberPassesFiltersExcludingUnit(member) {
  const columns = getMemberFilterColumns().filter(function(c){ return c.key !== 'unit'; });
  const filterState = summaryFilters.member;
  return columns.every(function(col) {
    return fieldPassesFilter(member, col.key, filterState, MEMBER_RANGE_FIELDS, MEMBER_MULTISELECT_FIELDS, MEMBER_TEXT_FIELDS);
  });
}

function getFilteredMobileSummary() {
  return mobilesData.filter(memberPassesFilters);
}

function switchSummaryTab(tab) {
  summaryActiveTab = tab;

  document.getElementById('tabBtnTable').classList.toggle('active', tab === 'table');
  document.getElementById('tabBtnChart').classList.toggle('active', tab === 'chart');
  document.getElementById('tabBtnUnit').classList.toggle('active', tab === 'unit');

  renderSummaryContent();
}

function renderSummaryContent() {
  if (summaryActiveTab === 'table') {
    renderSummaryTable();
  } else if (summaryActiveTab === 'chart') {
    renderSummaryChart();
  } else if (summaryActiveTab === 'unit') {
    renderSummaryByUnit();
  }
}

function renderSummaryTable() {
  const container = document.getElementById('summaryContent');

  if (summaryDataType === 'household') {
    const data = getFilteredHouseholdSummary();

    if (data.length === 0) {
      container.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center; padding:40px; color:#999;">ບໍ່ພົບຂໍ້ມູນຕາມເງື່ອນໄຂ</div>';
      return;
    }

  const visibleHouseholdCols = HOUSEHOLD_COLUMNS.filter(function(c){ return !hiddenColumns.household.has(c.key); });
    const visibleMemberCols = MEMBER_COLUMNS.filter(function(c){ return !hiddenColumns.member.has(c.key); });

    let html = '';
    data.forEach(item => {
      html += '<div class="summary-household-block">';
      html += '<div class="summary-household-header">🏠 ' + (item.household.name || '-') + ' (ລະຫັດ: ' + item.household.householdCode + ')</div>';

      html += '<table class="summary-table"><tbody>';
      for (let i = 0; i < visibleHouseholdCols.length; i += 2) {
        html += '<tr>';
        const col1 = visibleHouseholdCols[i];
        html += '<th style="width:15%;">' + col1.label + '</th><td style="width:35%;">' + (item.household[col1.key] || '-') + '</td>';
        if (visibleHouseholdCols[i+1]) {
          const col2 = visibleHouseholdCols[i+1];
          html += '<th style="width:15%;">' + col2.label + '</th><td style="width:35%;">' + (item.household[col2.key] || '-') + '</td>';
        } else {
          html += '<th></th><td></td>';
        }
        html += '</tr>';
      }
      html += '</tbody></table>';

   const ageRanges = getActiveAgeRanges();

      html += '<div style="padding:10px 15px; font-weight:600; color:#667eea;">👥 ສະມາຊິກ (' + item.members.length + ' คน)</div>';
      html += '<div style="overflow-x:auto;"><table class="summary-table"><thead><tr>';
      visibleMemberCols.forEach(col => {
        html += '<th>' + col.label + '</th>';
        if (col.key === 'age' && ageRanges.length > 0) {
          ageRanges.forEach(function(range) {
            html += '<th>ช่วง ' + formatAgeRangeLabel(range) + '</th>';
          });
        }
      });
      html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';
      item.members.forEach(m => {
        html += '<tr>';
        const memberAge = calculateAgeFromBirthDate(m.birthDate);
        visibleMemberCols.forEach(col => {
          let val = col.key === 'age' ? (memberAge ?? '-') : (m[col.key] || '-');
          html += '<td>' + val + '</td>';
          if (col.key === 'age' && ageRanges.length > 0) {
            ageRanges.forEach(function(range) {
              html += '<td style="text-align:center;">' + (isAgeInRange(memberAge, range) ? '✔️' : '') + '</td>';
            });
          }
        });
        html += '</tr>';
      });
      html += '</tbody></table></div>';
      html += '</div>';
    });

    container.innerHTML = html;

  } else {
    const data = getFilteredMobileSummary();

    if (data.length === 0) {
      container.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center; padding:40px; color:#999;">ບໍ່ພົบข้อมูลตามเงื่อนไข</div>';
      return;
    }

    const cols = [
      { key: 'name', label: 'ຊື່ ແລະ ນາມສະກຸນ' }, { key: 'gender', label: 'ເພດ' }, { key: 'birthDate', label: 'ວັນເດືອນປີເກີດ' },
      { key: 'ethnicity', label: 'ຊົນເຜົ່າ' }, { key: 'religion', label: 'ສາດສະໜາ' }, { key: 'education', label: 'ລະດັບການສຶກສາ' },
      { key: 'occupation', label: 'ອາຊີບ' }, { key: 'maritalStatus', label: 'ສະຖານະພາບແຕ່ງານ' }, { key: 'phone', label: 'ເບີໂທ' },
      { key: 'roomNumber', label: 'ຫ້ອງເບີ' }, { key: 'ownerName', label: 'ເຈົ້າຂອງຫ້ອງ' }, { key: 'rentalType', label: 'ປະເພດ' }
    ];

    let html = '<div style="overflow-x:auto;"><table class="summary-table"><thead><tr>';
    cols.forEach(c => { html += '<th>' + c.label + '</th>'; });
    html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';
    data.forEach(m => {
      html += '<tr>';
      cols.forEach(c => { html += '<td>' + (m[c.key] || '-') + '</td>'; });
      html += '</tr>';
    });
    html += '</tbody></table></div>';

    container.innerHTML = html;
  }
}
function renderSummaryChart() {
  const container = document.getElementById('summaryContent');

 summaryChartInstances.forEach(c => c.destroy());
  summaryChartInstances = [];
  chartInstancesMap = {};
  if (ageChartInstance) { ageChartInstance.destroy(); ageChartInstance = null; }

  let members = [];
  let householdItems = [];

  if (summaryDataType === 'household') {
    householdItems = getFilteredHouseholdSummary();
    householdItems.forEach(item => { members = members.concat(item.members); });
  } else {
    members = getFilteredMobileSummary();
  }

  if (members.length === 0 && householdItems.length === 0) {
    container.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center; padding:40px; color:#999;">ບໍ່ພົບຂໍ້ມູນຕາมเงื่อนไข</div>';
    return;
  }

  let html = '';

  if (summaryDataType === 'household') {
    const totalHouseholds = householdItems.length;
    const totalMembers = members.length;
    const totalRoofs = householdItems.reduce((sum, it) => sum + (parseInt(it.household.roofCount) || 0), 0);
    const avgFamilySize = totalHouseholds > 0 ? (totalMembers / totalHouseholds).toFixed(1) : 0;

    html += '<div class="summary-stat-cards">';
    html += '<div class="summary-stat-card"><div class="stat-icon">🏠</div><div class="stat-value">' + totalHouseholds + '</div><div class="stat-label">ຄອບຄົວ</div></div>';
    html += '<div class="summary-stat-card"><div class="stat-icon">👥</div><div class="stat-value">' + totalMembers + '</div><div class="stat-label">ຄົນທັງໝົດ</div></div>';
    html += '<div class="summary-stat-card"><div class="stat-icon">🏘️</div><div class="stat-value">' + totalRoofs + '</div><div class="stat-label">ຫຼັງຄາເຮືອນ</div></div>';
    html += '<div class="summary-stat-card"><div class="stat-icon">📊</div><div class="stat-value">' + avgFamilySize + '</div><div class="stat-label">ສະເລ່ຍ/ຄອບຄົວ</div></div>';
    html += '</div>';

    html += '<h3 style="margin:20px 0 10px; color:#4caf50;">🏠 ສະຫຼຸบข้อมูลหัวหน้าครอบครัว</h3>';
    html += '<div class="summary-chart-grid">';
    html += '<div class="summary-chart-box"><h4 style="text-align:center;">ການກະຈາຍຕາມໜ່ວຍ</h4><canvas id="chartUnit"></canvas></div>';
    html += '<div class="summary-chart-box"><h4 style="text-align:center;">ການມີສ່ວນຮ່ວມກັບບ້ານ</h4><canvas id="chartParticipation"></canvas></div>';
    html += '<div class="summary-chart-box"><h4 style="text-align:center;">ຂະໜາດຄອບຄົວ</h4><canvas id="chartFamilySize"></canvas></div>';
    html += '</div>';
  }

  html += '<h3 style="margin:20px 0 10px; color:#667eea;">👥 ສะหลุบข้อมูลสมาชิก</h3>';
  html += '<div class="summary-chart-grid">' +
    '<div class="summary-chart-box"><h4 style="text-align:center;">ເພດ</h4><canvas id="chartGender"></canvas></div>' +
    '<div class="summary-chart-box" id="ageChartBox">' +
      '<h4 style="text-align:center;">ຊ່ວງອາຍຸ <span style="font-size:11px; color:#999;">(ปรับได้)</span></h4>' +
      '<div id="ageChartControlsList" style="max-height:120px; overflow-y:auto; margin-bottom:8px;"></div>' +
      '<div style="display:flex; gap:6px; justify-content:center; margin-bottom:10px; flex-wrap:wrap;">' +
        '<button type="button" onclick="addAgeChartRangeRow()" style="padding:5px 10px; background:#4caf50; color:white; border:none; border-radius:6px; cursor:pointer; font-size:11px;">➕ ເพิ่มช่วง</button>' +
        '<button type="button" onclick="applyAgeChartRanges()" style="padding:5px 10px; background:#667eea; color:white; border:none; border-radius:6px; cursor:pointer; font-size:11px;">✅ ນຳໃຊ້</button>' +
        '<button type="button" onclick="resetAgeChartRanges()" style="padding:5px 10px; background:#607d8b; color:white; border:none; border-radius:6px; cursor:pointer; font-size:11px;">🔄 ค่าเริ่มต้น</button>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<canvas id="chartAge"></canvas>' +
    '</div>' +
    '<div class="summary-chart-box"><h4 style="text-align:center;">ຊົนเผ่า (Top 10)</h4><canvas id="chartEthnicity"></canvas></div>' +
    '<div class="summary-chart-box"><h4 style="text-align:center;">อาชีพ (Top 10)</h4><canvas id="chartOccupation"></canvas></div>' +
    '<div class="summary-chart-box"><h4 style="text-align:center;">ระดับการศึกษา</h4><canvas id="chartEducation"></canvas></div>' +
    '<div class="summary-chart-box"><h4 style="text-align:center;">ศาสนา</h4><canvas id="chartReligion"></canvas></div>';

  if (summaryDataType === 'mobile') {
    html += '<div class="summary-chart-box"><h4 style="text-align:center;">ประเภทการพักเช่า</h4><canvas id="chartRentalType"></canvas></div>';
  }

  html += '</div>';

  container.innerHTML = html;

  const countBy = (arr, key) => {
    const counts = {};
    arr.forEach(m => {
      const val = m[key] || 'ไม่ระบุ';
      counts[val] = (counts[val] || 0) + 1;
    });
    return counts;
  };
  const topN = (counts, n) => Object.entries(counts).sort((a,b) => b[1]-a[1]).slice(0, n);
  const colors = ['#667eea','#764ba2','#4caf50','#ff9800','#f44336','#2196f3','#e91e63','#009688','#ffc107','#9c27b0'];

 function makePie(canvasId, counts) {
    const canvas = document.getElementById(canvasId);
    if (!canvas) return;
    const entries = Object.entries(counts);
    const chart = new Chart(canvas, {
      type: 'pie',
      data: { labels: entries.map(e => e[0]), datasets: [{ data: entries.map(e => e[1]), backgroundColor: colors }] },
      options: { responsive: true, plugins: { legend: { position: 'bottom' } } }
    });
    summaryChartInstances.push(chart);
    chartInstancesMap[canvasId] = chart;
  }

  function makeBar(canvasId, entries, label) {
    const canvas = document.getElementById(canvasId);
    if (!canvas) return;
    const chart = new Chart(canvas, {
      type: 'bar',
      data: { labels: entries.map(e => e[0]), datasets: [{ label: label || 'ຈໍານວນ (ຄົນ)', data: entries.map(e => e[1]), backgroundColor: '#667eea' }] },
      options: { responsive: true, plugins: { legend: { display: false } } }
    });
    summaryChartInstances.push(chart);
    chartInstancesMap[canvasId] = chart;
  }

  if (summaryDataType === 'household') {
    const unitCounts = {};
    householdItems.forEach(item => {
      const u = item.household.unit || 'ไม่ระบุ';
      unitCounts[u] = (unitCounts[u] || 0) + 1;
    });
    makeBar('chartUnit', Object.entries(unitCounts).sort((a,b) => naturalUnitCompare(a[0], b[0])), 'จำนวนครอบครัว');

    const participationLabels = {
      excellent: '⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ ดีเลิศ',
      verygood: '⭐⭐⭐⭐ ดีมาก',
      moderate: '⭐⭐⭐ ปานกลาง',
      lacking: '⭐ ขาดการมีส่วนร่วม'
    };
    const participationCounts = {};
    householdItems.forEach(item => {
      const key = item.household.villageParticipation;
      const lbl = participationLabels[key] || 'ไม่ระบุ';
      participationCounts[lbl] = (participationCounts[lbl] || 0) + 1;
    });
    makePie('chartParticipation', participationCounts);

    const sizeGroups = { '1-2 คน':0, '3-4 คน':0, '5-6 คน':0, '7+ คน':0 };
    householdItems.forEach(item => {
      const size = item.members.length;
      if (size <= 2) sizeGroups['1-2 คน']++;
      else if (size <= 4) sizeGroups['3-4 คน']++;
      else if (size <= 6) sizeGroups['5-6 คน']++;
      else sizeGroups['7+ คน']++;
    });
    makeBar('chartFamilySize', Object.entries(sizeGroups), 'จำนวนครอบครัว');
  }

  makePie('chartGender', countBy(members, 'gender'));
  makePie('chartEducation', countBy(members, 'education'));
  makePie('chartReligion', countBy(members, 'religion'));
  makeBar('chartEthnicity', topN(countBy(members, 'ethnicity'), 10));
  makeBar('chartOccupation', topN(countBy(members, 'occupation'), 10));

  if (summaryDataType === 'mobile') {
    makePie('chartRentalType', countBy(members, 'rentalType'));
  }

  renderAgeChartControls();
  renderAgeChart(members);
}

// ===================================
// AGE CHART - ปรับช่วงอายุได้เอง
// ===================================
function getDefaultAgeChartRanges() {
  return [
    { label: '0-5', min: 0, max: 5 },
    { label: '6-12', min: 6, max: 12 },
    { label: '13-18', min: 13, max: 18 },
    { label: '19-30', min: 19, max: 30 },
    { label: '31-45', min: 31, max: 45 },
    { label: '46-60', min: 46, max: 60 },
    { label: '60+', min: 61, max: 999 }
  ];
}

function buildAgeChartRangeRowHTML(range) {
  return '<div class="age-chart-range-row" style="display:flex; gap:5px; align-items:center; margin-bottom:5px;">' +
    '<input type="text" class="acr-label" value="' + (range.label || '') + '" placeholder="ป้ายชื่อ" style="width:65px; padding:5px; border:1px solid #ddd; border-radius:4px; font-size:12px;">' +
    '<input type="number" class="acr-min" value="' + (range.min !== undefined && range.min !== '' ? range.min : '') + '" placeholder="ต่ำสุด" style="width:55px; padding:5px; border:1px solid #ddd; border-radius:4px; font-size:12px;">' +
    '<span style="font-size:12px;">-</span>' +
    '<input type="number" class="acr-max" value="' + (range.max !== undefined && range.max !== '' ? range.max : '') + '" placeholder="สูงสุด" style="width:55px; padding:5px; border:1px solid #ddd; border-radius:4px; font-size:12px;">' +
    '<button type="button" onclick="removeAgeChartRangeRow(this)" style="padding:4px 8px; background:#f44336; color:white; border:none; border-radius:4px; cursor:pointer; font-size:11px;">✖</button>' +
    '</div>';
}

function renderAgeChartControls() {
  const list = document.getElementById('ageChartControlsList');
  if (!list) return;
  const ranges = customAgeChartRanges || getDefaultAgeChartRanges();
  list.innerHTML = ranges.map(function(r) { return buildAgeChartRangeRowHTML(r); }).join('');
}

function addAgeChartRangeRow() {
  const list = document.getElementById('ageChartControlsList');
  if (!list) return;
  list.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', buildAgeChartRangeRowHTML({ label: '', min: '', max: '' }));
}

function removeAgeChartRangeRow(btn) {
  const row = btn.closest('.age-chart-range-row');
  if (row) row.remove();
}

function applyAgeChartRanges() {
  const list = document.getElementById('ageChartControlsList');
  if (!list) return;
  const rows = list.querySelectorAll('.age-chart-range-row');
  const newRanges = [];
  rows.forEach(function(row) {
    const label = row.querySelector('.acr-label').value.trim();
    const min = row.querySelector('.acr-min').value;
    const max = row.querySelector('.acr-max').value;
    if (min !== '' && max !== '') {
      newRanges.push({
        label: label || (min + '-' + max),
        min: parseInt(min),
        max: parseInt(max)
      });
    }
  });
  if (newRanges.length === 0) {
    showToast('ກະลุนาระบุอย่างน้อย 1 ช่วงอายุ', 'error');
    return;
  }
  customAgeChartRanges = newRanges;
  redrawAgeChartOnly();
  showToast('นำใช้ช่วงอายุใหม่สำเร็จ', 'success');
}

function resetAgeChartRanges() {
  customAgeChartRanges = null;
  renderAgeChartControls();
  redrawAgeChartOnly();
  showToast('คืนค่าช่วงอายุเริ่มต้นสำเร็จ', 'success');
}

function redrawAgeChartOnly() {
  let members = [];
  if (summaryDataType === 'household') {
    getFilteredHouseholdSummary().forEach(item => { members = members.concat(item.members); });
  } else {
    members = getFilteredMobileSummary();
  }
  renderAgeChart(members);
}

function renderAgeChart(members) {
  if (ageChartInstance) {
    ageChartInstance.destroy();
    ageChartInstance = null;
  }
  const canvas = document.getElementById('chartAge');
  if (!canvas) return;

  const ranges = customAgeChartRanges || getDefaultAgeChartRanges();
  const counts = {};
  ranges.forEach(function(r) { counts[r.label] = 0; });
  counts['ไม่ระบุ'] = 0;

  members.forEach(function(m) {
    const age = calculateAgeFromBirthDate(m.birthDate);
    if (age === null) { counts['ไม่ระบุ']++; return; }
    let matched = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < ranges.length; i++) {
      if (age >= ranges[i].min && age <= ranges[i].max) {
        counts[ranges[i].label]++;
        matched = true;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (!matched) counts['ไม่ระบุ']++;
  });

const entries = Object.entries(counts);
  ageChartInstance = new Chart(canvas, {
    type: 'bar',
    data: { labels: entries.map(e => e[0]), datasets: [{ label: 'จำนวน (คน)', data: entries.map(e => e[1]), backgroundColor: '#667eea' }] },
    options: { responsive: true, plugins: { legend: { display: false } } }
  });
  chartInstancesMap['chartAge'] = ageChartInstance;
}

function renderSummaryByUnit() {
  const container = document.getElementById('summaryContent');
  let unitGroups = {};

  if (summaryDataType === 'household') {
    const data = getFilteredHouseholdSummary();

    if (data.length === 0) {
      container.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center; padding:40px; color:#999;">ບໍ່ພົບຂໍ້ມູນຕາມເງື່ອນໄຂ</div>';
      return;
    }

    data.forEach(item => {
      const unit = item.household.unit || 'ບໍ່ລະບຸໜ່ວຍ';
      if (!unitGroups[unit]) unitGroups[unit] = [];
      unitGroups[unit].push(item);
    });

    let html = '';
   Object.keys(unitGroups).sort(naturalUnitCompare).forEach(unit => {
      const items = unitGroups[unit];
      const totalMembers = items.reduce((sum, it) => sum + it.members.length, 0);

      html += '<div class="summary-unit-group">';
      html += '<div class="summary-unit-group-title">📍 ໜ່ວຍ: ' + unit + ' — ' + items.length + ' ຄອບຄົວ, ' + totalMembers + ' ຄົນ</div>';

      items.forEach(item => {
        html += '<div style="background:white; border-radius:8px; padding:10px; margin-bottom:8px;">';
        html += '<strong>🏠 ' + (item.household.name || '-') + '</strong> (ລະຫັດ: ' + item.household.householdCode + ')';
        html += '<ul style="margin:8px 0 0 20px;">';
        item.members.forEach(m => {
          const age = calculateAgeFromBirthDate(m.birthDate);
          html += '<li>' + m.name + ' — ' + (m.relationship || '-') + ', ' + (m.gender || '-') + (age !== null ? ', ' + age + ' ປີ' : '') + '</li>';
        });
        html += '</ul></div>';
      });

      html += '</div>';
    });

    container.innerHTML = html;

  } else {
    const data = getFilteredMobileSummary();

    if (data.length === 0) {
      container.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center; padding:40px; color:#999;">ບໍ່ພົບຂໍ້ມູນຕາມເງື່ອນໄຂ</div>';
      return;
    }

    data.forEach(m => {
      const unit = m.unit || 'ไม่ระบุหน่วย';
      if (!unitGroups[unit]) unitGroups[unit] = [];
      unitGroups[unit].push(m);
    });

    let html = '';
   Object.keys(unitGroups).sort(naturalUnitCompare).forEach(unit => {
      const items = unitGroups[unit];
      html += '<div class="summary-unit-group">';
      html += '<div class="summary-unit-group-title">📍 ໜ່ວຍ: ' + unit + ' — ' + items.length + ' ຄົນ</div>';
      html += '<ul style="margin:0 0 0 20px;">';
      items.forEach(m => {
        const age = calculateAgeFromBirthDate(m.birthDate);
        html += '<li>' + m.name + ' — ' + (m.gender || '-') + (age !== null ? ', ' + age + ' ปี' : '') + ', ' + (m.roomNumber || '-') + '</li>';
      });
      html += '</ul></div>';
    });

    container.innerHTML = html;
  }
}
function countByGlobal(arr, key) {
  const counts = {};
  arr.forEach(function(m) {
    const val = m[key] || 'ບໍ່ລະບຸ';
    counts[val] = (counts[val] || 0) + 1;
  });
  return counts;
}

function generateReportHTMLForActiveTab() {
  if (summaryActiveTab === 'chart') {
    return generateChartReportHTML();
  } else if (summaryActiveTab === 'unit') {
    return generateUnitReportHTML();
  }
  return generateReportHTML();
}

function generateChartReportHTML() {
  let title = summaryDataType === 'household' ? 'ສรุปข้อมูลແบบชาร์ต - ปะชากอนคงที่' : 'ສรุปข้อมูลແบบชาร์ต - ปะชากอนเคลื่อนที่';
  let html = '<div style="font-family: \'Phetsarath OT\', \'Noto Sans Lao\', Arial, sans-serif; padding: 20px;">';
  html += '<h1 style="text-align:center; color:#667eea;">' + title + '</h1>';
  html += '<p style="text-align:center; color:#666;">วันที่พิมพ์: ' + new Date().toLocaleDateString('lo-LA') + '</p>';

  const statCardsEl = document.querySelector('#summaryContent .summary-stat-cards');
  if (statCardsEl) {
    html += '<div style="margin:20px 0;">' + statCardsEl.outerHTML + '</div>';
  }

  const chartBoxes = document.querySelectorAll('#summaryContent .summary-chart-box');
  html += '<div style="display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:20px; margin-top:20px;">';
  chartBoxes.forEach(function(box) {
    const heading = box.querySelector('h4');
    const canvas = box.querySelector('canvas');
    if (!canvas) return;
    const chart = chartInstancesMap[canvas.id];
    if (!chart) return;
    let imgData = '';
    try { imgData = chart.toBase64Image(); } catch (e) { return; }
    html += '<div style="flex:1; min-width:300px; border:1px solid #ddd; border-radius:10px; padding:10px; page-break-inside:avoid;">';
    html += '<h4 style="text-align:center; color:#667eea;">' + (heading ? heading.textContent.replace('(ปรับได้)','').trim() : '') + '</h4>';
    html += '<img src="' + imgData + '" style="width:100%; display:block;">';
    html += '</div>';
  });
  html += '</div></div>';

  return html;
}

function generateUnitReportHTML() {
  const summaryContentEl = document.getElementById('summaryContent');
  let title = summaryDataType === 'household' ? 'ລາຍຊື່ແຕ່ລະຄອບຄົວຕາມໜ່ວຍຂອງປະຊາກອນຄົງທີ່' : 'ລາຍຊື່ແຕ່ລະຄອບຄົວຕາມໜ່ວຍຂອງປະຊາກອນຄົງທີ່';
  let html = '<div style="font-family: \'Phetsarath OT\', \'Noto Sans Lao\', Arial, sans-serif; padding: 20px;">';
  html += '<h1 style="text-align:center; color:#667eea;">' + title + '</h1>';
  html += '<p style="text-align:center; color:#666;">ວັນທີພິມ: ' + new Date().toLocaleDateString('lo-LA') + '</p>';
  html += summaryContentEl ? summaryContentEl.innerHTML : '<p>ບໍ່ມີຂໍ້ມູນ</p>';
  html += '</div>';
  return html;
}

function buildChartExcelWorkbook(wb) {
  let members = [];
  let householdItems = [];
  if (summaryDataType === 'household') {
    householdItems = getFilteredHouseholdSummary();
    householdItems.forEach(function(item) { members = members.concat(item.members); });

    const unitCounts = {};
    householdItems.forEach(function(item) {
      const u = item.household.unit || 'ไม่ระบุ';
      unitCounts[u] = (unitCounts[u] || 0) + 1;
    });
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(Object.entries(unitCounts).map(function(e){ return { 'หน่วย': e[0], 'จำนวนครอบครัว': e[1] }; })), 'ตามหน่วย');

    const participationLabels = { excellent:'ดีเลิศ', verygood:'ดีมาก', moderate:'ปานกลาง', lacking:'ขาดการมีส่วนร่วม' };
    const partCounts = {};
    householdItems.forEach(function(item) {
      const lbl = participationLabels[item.household.villageParticipation] || 'ไม่ระบุ';
      partCounts[lbl] = (partCounts[lbl] || 0) + 1;
    });
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(Object.entries(partCounts).map(function(e){ return { 'ระดับการมีส่วนร่วม': e[0], 'จำนวนครอบครัว': e[1] }; })), 'การมีส่วนร่วม');
  } else {
    members = getFilteredMobileSummary();
  }

  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(Object.entries(countByGlobal(members,'gender')).map(function(e){ return {'เพศ': e[0], 'จำนวน': e[1]}; })), 'เพศ');
  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(Object.entries(countByGlobal(members,'education')).map(function(e){ return {'การศึกษา': e[0], 'จำนวน': e[1]}; })), 'การศึกษา');
  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(Object.entries(countByGlobal(members,'religion')).map(function(e){ return {'ศาสนา': e[0], 'จำนวน': e[1]}; })), 'ศาสนา');
  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(Object.entries(countByGlobal(members,'ethnicity')).map(function(e){ return {'ชนเผ่า': e[0], 'จำนวน': e[1]}; })), 'ชนเผ่า');
  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(Object.entries(countByGlobal(members,'occupation')).map(function(e){ return {'อาชีพ': e[0], 'จำนวน': e[1]}; })), 'อาชีพ');

  const ranges = customAgeChartRanges || getDefaultAgeChartRanges();
  const ageCounts = {};
  ranges.forEach(function(r){ ageCounts[r.label] = 0; });
  ageCounts['ไม่ระบุ'] = 0;
  members.forEach(function(m) {
    const age = calculateAgeFromBirthDate(m.birthDate);
    if (age === null) { ageCounts['ไม่ระบุ']++; return; }
    let matched = false;
    for (let i=0;i<ranges.length;i++) {
      if (age >= ranges[i].min && age <= ranges[i].max) { ageCounts[ranges[i].label]++; matched = true; break; }
    }
    if (!matched) ageCounts['ไม่ระบุ']++;
  });
  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(Object.entries(ageCounts).map(function(e){ return {'ช่วงอายุ': e[0], 'จำนวน': e[1]}; })), 'ช่วงอายุ');
}

function buildUnitExcelWorkbook(wb) {
  const rows = [];
  if (summaryDataType === 'household') {
    const data = getFilteredHouseholdSummary();
    data.forEach(function(item) {
      const unit = item.household.unit || 'ไม่ระบุ';
      item.members.forEach(function(m) {
        const age = calculateAgeFromBirthDate(m.birthDate);
        rows.push({
          'หน่วย': unit,
          'ครอบครัว': item.household.name || '-',
          'ชื่อสมาชิก': m.name || '-',
          'สายพัวพัน': m.relationship || '-',
          'เพศ': m.gender || '-',
          'อายุ': age !== null ? age : '-'
        });
      });
    });
  } else {
    const data = getFilteredMobileSummary();
    data.forEach(function(m) {
      const age = calculateAgeFromBirthDate(m.birthDate);
      rows.push({
        'หน่วย': m.unit || 'ไม่ระบุ',
        'ชื่อ': m.name || '-',
        'เพศ': m.gender || '-',
        'อายุ': age !== null ? age : '-',
        'ห้องเบอร์': m.roomNumber || '-'
      });
    });
  }
  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(rows), 'ตามหน่วย');
}
function generateReportHTML() {
  let title = summaryDataType === 'household' ? 'ສรุปข้อมูลปะชากอนคงที่' : 'ສรุปข้อมูลปะชากอนเคลื่อนที่';
  let html = '<div style="font-family: \'Phetsarath OT\', \'Noto Sans Lao\', Arial, sans-serif; padding: 20px;">';
  html += '<h1 style="text-align:center; color:#667eea;">' + title + '</h1>';
  html += '<p style="text-align:center; color:#666;">วันที่พิมพ์: ' + new Date().toLocaleDateString('lo-LA') + '</p>';

  if (summaryDataType === 'household') {
    const data = getFilteredHouseholdSummary();
    const visibleHouseholdCols = HOUSEHOLD_COLUMNS.filter(function(c){ return !hiddenColumns.household.has(c.key); });
    const visibleMemberCols = MEMBER_COLUMNS.filter(function(c){ return !hiddenColumns.member.has(c.key); });

  if (currentFilterScope === 'member') {
      let allMembers = [];
      data.forEach(function(item) { allMembers = allMembers.concat(item.members); });
      const ageRangesRpt1 = getActiveAgeRanges();

      html += '<table style="width:100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size:12px;"><thead><tr>';
      visibleMemberCols.forEach(function(col) {
        html += '<th style="border:1px solid #ccc; padding:5px; background:#667eea; color:white;">' + col.label + '</th>';
        if (col.key === 'age' && ageRangesRpt1.length > 0) {
          ageRangesRpt1.forEach(function(range) {
            html += '<th style="border:1px solid #ccc; padding:5px; background:#667eea; color:white;">ช่วง ' + formatAgeRangeLabel(range) + '</th>';
          });
        }
      });
      html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';
      allMembers.forEach(function(m) {
        html += '<tr>';
        const mAge = calculateAgeFromBirthDate(m.birthDate);
        visibleMemberCols.forEach(function(col) {
          let val = col.key === 'age' ? (mAge ?? '-') : (m[col.key] || '-');
          html += '<td style="border:1px solid #ccc; padding:4px;">' + val + '</td>';
          if (col.key === 'age' && ageRangesRpt1.length > 0) {
            ageRangesRpt1.forEach(function(range) {
              html += '<td style="border:1px solid #ccc; padding:4px; text-align:center;">' + (isAgeInRange(mAge, range) ? '✔️' : '') + '</td>';
            });
          }
        });
        html += '</tr>';
      });
      html += '</tbody></table>';

    } else if (currentFilterScope === 'household') {
      html += '<table style="width:100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size:12px;"><thead><tr>';
      visibleHouseholdCols.forEach(function(col) { html += '<th style="border:1px solid #ccc; padding:5px; background:#667eea; color:white;">' + col.label + '</th>'; });
      html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';
      data.forEach(function(item) {
        html += '<tr>';
        visibleHouseholdCols.forEach(function(col) {
          html += '<td style="border:1px solid #ccc; padding:4px;">' + (item.household[col.key] || '-') + '</td>';
        });
        html += '</tr>';
      });
      html += '</tbody></table>';

    } else {
      data.forEach(item => {
        html += '<div style="page-break-inside: avoid; margin-bottom: 30px; border: 1px solid #999;">';
        html += '<div style="background:#e8f5e9; padding:10px; font-weight:bold; font-size:16px;">🏠 ' + (item.household.name || '-') + ' (รหัส: ' + item.household.householdCode + ')</div>';

        html += '<table style="width:100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size:13px;">';
        for (let i = 0; i < visibleHouseholdCols.length; i += 2) {
          html += '<tr>';
          const col1 = visibleHouseholdCols[i];
          html += '<th style="border:1px solid #ccc; padding:5px; background:#f0f0f0; text-align:left; width:15%;">' + col1.label + '</th><td style="border:1px solid #ccc; padding:5px; width:35%;">' + (item.household[col1.key] || '-') + '</td>';
          if (visibleHouseholdCols[i+1]) {
            const col2 = visibleHouseholdCols[i+1];
            html += '<th style="border:1px solid #ccc; padding:5px; background:#f0f0f0; text-align:left; width:15%;">' + col2.label + '</th><td style="border:1px solid #ccc; padding:5px; width:35%;">' + (item.household[col2.key] || '-') + '</td>';
          } else {
            html += '<th style="border:1px solid #ccc;"></th><td style="border:1px solid #ccc;"></td>';
          }
          html += '</tr>';
        }
        html += '</table>';

  const ageRangesRpt2 = getActiveAgeRanges();

        html += '<div style="padding:8px; font-weight:bold; background:#f8f9ff;">👥 ສະມາຊິກ (' + item.members.length + ' คน)</div>';
        html += '<table style="width:100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size:12px;"><thead><tr>';
        visibleMemberCols.forEach(col => {
          html += '<th style="border:1px solid #ccc; padding:4px; background:#667eea; color:white;">' + col.label + '</th>';
          if (col.key === 'age' && ageRangesRpt2.length > 0) {
            ageRangesRpt2.forEach(function(range) {
              html += '<th style="border:1px solid #ccc; padding:4px; background:#667eea; color:white;">ช่วง ' + formatAgeRangeLabel(range) + '</th>';
            });
          }
        });
        html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';
        item.members.forEach(m => {
          html += '<tr>';
          const mAge2 = calculateAgeFromBirthDate(m.birthDate);
          visibleMemberCols.forEach(col => {
            let val = col.key === 'age' ? (mAge2 ?? '-') : (m[col.key] || '-');
            html += '<td style="border:1px solid #ccc; padding:4px;">' + val + '</td>';
            if (col.key === 'age' && ageRangesRpt2.length > 0) {
              ageRangesRpt2.forEach(function(range) {
                html += '<td style="border:1px solid #ccc; padding:4px; text-align:center;">' + (isAgeInRange(mAge2, range) ? '✔️' : '') + '</td>';
              });
            }
          });
          html += '</tr>';
        });
        html += '</tbody></table>';
        html += '</div>';
      });
    }

  } else {
    const data = getFilteredMobileSummary();
    const cols = [
      { key: 'name', label: 'ชื่อ' }, { key: 'gender', label: 'เพศ' }, { key: 'birthDate', label: 'วันเกิด' },
      { key: 'ethnicity', label: 'ชนเผ่า' }, { key: 'religion', label: 'ศาสนา' }, { key: 'education', label: 'การศึกษา' },
      { key: 'occupation', label: 'อาชีพ' }, { key: 'maritalStatus', label: 'สถานะภาพ' }, { key: 'phone', label: 'เบอร์โทร' },
      { key: 'roomNumber', label: 'ห้องเบอร์' }, { key: 'ownerName', label: 'เจ้าของห้อง' }, { key: 'rentalType', label: 'ประเภท' }
    ];

    html += '<table style="width:100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size:12px;"><thead><tr>';
    cols.forEach(c => { html += '<th style="border:1px solid #ccc; padding:5px; background:#667eea; color:white;">' + c.label + '</th>'; });
    html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';
    data.forEach(m => {
      html += '<tr>';
      cols.forEach(c => { html += '<td style="border:1px solid #ccc; padding:5px;">' + (m[c.key] || '-') + '</td>'; });
      html += '</tr>';
    });
    html += '</tbody></table>';
  }

  html += '</div>';
  return html;
}
async function downloadExcel() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('btnDownloadExcel');
  if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.style.opacity = '0.6'; btn.style.cursor = 'not-allowed'; }
  showDownloadProgress('ກຳລັງສ້າງ Excel...', '📊');

  try {
    updateDownloadProgress(5, 'ກຳລັງກະກຽມຂໍ້ມູນ...');
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 50));

   const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();

    if (summaryActiveTab === 'chart') {
      updateDownloadProgress(30, 'ກຳລັງສ້າງຂໍ້ມູນແບบชาร์ต...');
      buildChartExcelWorkbook(wb);
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 100));

    } else if (summaryActiveTab === 'unit') {
      updateDownloadProgress(30, 'ກຳລັງສ້າງຂໍ້มูลตามหน่วย...');
      buildUnitExcelWorkbook(wb);
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 100));

    } else if (summaryDataType === 'household') {
      const data = getFilteredHouseholdSummary();
      const visibleHouseholdCols = HOUSEHOLD_COLUMNS.filter(function(c){ return !hiddenColumns.household.has(c.key); });
      const visibleMemberCols = MEMBER_COLUMNS.filter(function(c){ return !hiddenColumns.member.has(c.key); });

      if (currentFilterScope === 'household' || currentFilterScope === 'both') {
        updateDownloadProgress(20, 'ກຳລັງສ້າງຕາຕະລາງຫົວໜ້າຄອບຄົວ...');
        const householdRows = [];
        const chunkSize = 200;
        for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i += chunkSize) {
          const chunk = data.slice(i, i + chunkSize);
          chunk.forEach(item => {
            const row = {};
            visibleHouseholdCols.forEach(col => { row[col.label] = item.household[col.key] || ''; });
            householdRows.push(row);
          });
          const pct = 20 + Math.round((i / Math.max(data.length, 1)) * 25);
          updateDownloadProgress(pct, 'ກຳລັງສ້າງຕາຕະລາງຫົວໜ້າຄອບຄົວ... (' + Math.min(i + chunkSize, data.length) + '/' + data.length + ')');
          await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 0));
        }
        const wsHousehold = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(householdRows);
        XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, wsHousehold, 'หัวหน้าครอบครัว');
      }

      if (currentFilterScope === 'member' || currentFilterScope === 'both') {
        updateDownloadProgress(50, 'ກຳລັງສ້າງຕາຕະລາງສະມາຊິກ...');
        const ageRangesXlsx = getActiveAgeRanges();
        let allMembersFlat = [];
        data.forEach(item => { allMembersFlat = allMembersFlat.concat(item.members); });

        const memberRows = [];
        const chunkSize = 200;
        for (let i = 0; i < allMembersFlat.length; i += chunkSize) {
          const chunk = allMembersFlat.slice(i, i + chunkSize);
          chunk.forEach(m => {
            const row = {};
            const memberAge = calculateAgeFromBirthDate(m.birthDate);
            visibleMemberCols.forEach(col => {
              row[col.label] = col.key === 'age' ? (memberAge ?? '') : (m[col.key] || '');
              if (col.key === 'age' && ageRangesXlsx.length > 0) {
                ageRangesXlsx.forEach(function(range) {
                  row['ช่วง ' + formatAgeRangeLabel(range)] = isAgeInRange(memberAge, range) ? 'X' : '';
                });
              }
            });
            memberRows.push(row);
          });
          const pct = 50 + Math.round((i / Math.max(allMembersFlat.length, 1)) * 35);
          updateDownloadProgress(pct, 'ກຳລັງສ້າງຕາຕະລາງສະມາຊິກ... (' + Math.min(i + chunkSize, allMembersFlat.length) + '/' + allMembersFlat.length + ')');
          await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 0));
        }
        const wsMembers = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(memberRows);
        XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, wsMembers, 'สมาชิก');
      }

    } else {
      const data = getFilteredMobileSummary();
      updateDownloadProgress(30, 'ກຳລັງສ້າງຕາຕະລາງ...');
      const rows = data.map(m => ({
        'ชื่อ': m.name || '', 'เพศ': m.gender || '', 'วันเกิด': m.birthDate || '',
        'ชนเผ่า': m.ethnicity || '', 'ศาสนา': m.religion || '', 'การศึกษา': m.education || '',
        'อาชีพ': m.occupation || '', 'สถานะภาพ': m.maritalStatus || '', 'เบอร์โทร': m.phone || '',
        'ห้องเบอร์': m.roomNumber || '', 'เจ้าของห้อง': m.ownerName || '', 'ประเภท': m.rentalType || ''
      }));
      const ws = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(rows);
      XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, 'ปะชากอนเคลื่อนที่');
    }

    updateDownloadProgress(90, 'ກຳລັງບັນທຶກໄຟລ໌...');
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 100));

    const fileName = 'สรุปข้อมูล_' + new Date().getTime() + '.xlsx';
    XLSX.writeFile(wb, fileName);

    updateDownloadProgress(100, 'ດາວໂຫລดสำเร็จ!');
    showToast('ດາວໂຫລด Excel สำเร็จ', 'success');
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Excel export error:', error);
    showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาดในการสร้าง Excel: ' + error.message, 'error');
  } finally {
    hideDownloadProgress();
    if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.style.opacity = '1'; btn.style.cursor = 'pointer'; }
  }
}

async function downloadWord() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('btnDownloadWord');
  if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.style.opacity = '0.6'; btn.style.cursor = 'not-allowed'; }
  showDownloadProgress('ກຳລັງສ້າງ Word...', '📝');

  try {
    updateDownloadProgress(20, 'ກຳລັງກະກຽມຂໍ້ມູນ...');
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 50));

   const reportHTML = generateReportHTMLForActiveTab();
    updateDownloadProgress(60, 'ກຳລັງສ້າງໄຟລ໌...');
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 50));

    const fullHTML = '<html xmlns:o="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:office" ' +
      'xmlns:w="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:word" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/TR/REC-html40">' +
      '<head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>ສรุปข้อมูล</title></head>' +
      '<body>' + reportHTML + '</body></html>';

    const blob = new Blob(['\ufeff', fullHTML], { type: 'application/msword' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = 'สรุปข้อมูล_' + new Date().getTime() + '.doc';
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

    updateDownloadProgress(100, 'ດາວໂຫລดสำเร็จ!');
    showToast('ດາວໂຫລด Word สำเร็จ', 'success');
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Word export error:', error);
    showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาดในการสร้าง Word: ' + error.message, 'error');
  } finally {
    hideDownloadProgress();
    if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.style.opacity = '1'; btn.style.cursor = 'pointer'; }
  }
}

function printReport() {
  const reportHTML = generateReportHTML();
  const printWindow = window.open('', '_blank');
  printWindow.document.write(
    '<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>ພິมเอกสาร</title>' +
    '<style>body { font-family: "Phetsarath OT", "Noto Sans Lao", Arial, sans-serif; }' +
    '@media print { @page { size: A4; margin: 15mm; } }</style></head><body>' + reportHTML + '</body></html>'
  );
  printWindow.document.close();

  setTimeout(() => {
    printWindow.focus();
    printWindow.print();
  }, 500);
}
async function downloadPDF() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('btnDownloadPDF');
  if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.style.opacity = '0.6'; btn.style.cursor = 'not-allowed'; }
  showDownloadProgress('ກຳລັງສ້າງ PDF...', '📄');

  let container = null;
  let renderDiv = null;

  try {
    if (typeof html2canvas === 'undefined' || !window.jspdf || !window.jspdf.jsPDF) {
      throw new Error('ບໍ່ພົบ library ທີ່ຈຳເປັນ ກະລຸນາ Refresh ໜ້າເວັບແລ້ວລອງໃໝ່');
    }

    updateDownloadProgress(5, 'ກຳລັງກະກຽມຂໍ້ມູນ...');
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 50));

    const reportHTML = generateReportHTMLForActiveTab();

    container = document.createElement('div');
    container.innerHTML = reportHTML;
    container.style.cssText = 'position: absolute; left: -9999px; top: 0; width: 1400px; background: white; margin: 0; padding: 0;';
    document.body.appendChild(container);

    updateDownloadProgress(15, 'ກຳລັງກະກຽມ Layout...');

    if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) {
      await document.fonts.ready;
    }
    await new Promise(r => requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(r)));
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 300));

    const rootDiv = container.firstElementChild || container;

    const headerEls = [];
    const bodyEls = [];
    Array.from(rootDiv.children).forEach(function(el) {
      if ((el.tagName === 'H1' || el.tagName === 'P') && bodyEls.length === 0) {
        headerEls.push(el);
      } else {
        bodyEls.push(el);
      }
    });

    let chunks = [];
    if (bodyEls.length === 1 && bodyEls[0].tagName === 'TABLE') {
      chunks = splitTableIntoChunks(bodyEls[0], 40);
    } else if (bodyEls.length === 1 && bodyEls[0].querySelector && bodyEls[0].querySelector('table')) {
      const innerTable = bodyEls[0].querySelector('table');
      chunks = splitTableIntoChunks(innerTable, 40);
    } else {
      const BLOCKS_PER_CHUNK = 6;
      for (let i = 0; i < bodyEls.length; i += BLOCKS_PER_CHUNK) {
        chunks.push(bodyEls.slice(i, i + BLOCKS_PER_CHUNK));
      }
    }
    if (chunks.length === 0) chunks = [bodyEls];

    updateDownloadProgress(20, 'ກຳລັງແບ່ງໜ້າ PDF (' + chunks.length + ' ຊຸດ)...');

    renderDiv = document.createElement('div');
    renderDiv.style.cssText = 'position: absolute; left: -9999px; top: 0; width: 1400px; background: white; padding: 15px; font-family: "Phetsarath OT","Noto Sans Lao",Arial,sans-serif;';
    document.body.appendChild(renderDiv);

const margin = 10;
    const jsPDFCtor = window.jspdf.jsPDF;
    const pdf = new jsPDFCtor({ unit: 'mm', format: 'a4', orientation: 'landscape' });
    const usableWidth = pdf.internal.pageSize.getWidth() - margin * 2;

    let pagesAdded = 0;
    let skippedChunks = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < chunks.length; i++) {
      renderDiv.innerHTML = '';

      if (i === 0) {
        headerEls.forEach(function(el) { renderDiv.appendChild(el.cloneNode(true)); });
      }

      const chunkEls = chunks[i];
      if (Array.isArray(chunkEls)) {
        chunkEls.forEach(function(el) { renderDiv.appendChild(el.cloneNode(true)); });
      } else if (chunkEls) {
        renderDiv.appendChild(chunkEls.cloneNode(true));
      }

      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 30));

      try {
        const canvas = await html2canvas(renderDiv, {
          scale: 1.5,
          useCORS: true,
          backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
          logging: false
        });

        if (!canvas || !canvas.width || !canvas.height) {
          console.warn('ຂ້າມຊຸດທີ່ ' + (i + 1) + ' — canvas ວ່າງເປົ່າ');
          skippedChunks++;
        } else {
const imgData = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.95);
          let imgHeightMM = (canvas.height * usableWidth) / canvas.width;
          if (!imgHeightMM || isNaN(imgHeightMM) || imgHeightMM <= 0) {
            imgHeightMM = usableWidth * 0.6;
          }

          if (pagesAdded > 0) pdf.addPage();
          pdf.addImage(imgData, 'JPEG', margin, margin, usableWidth, imgHeightMM);
          pagesAdded++;
        }
      } catch (chunkError) {
        console.error('ຂ້າມຊຸດທີ່ ' + (i + 1) + ' ເນື່ອງຈາກ error:', chunkError);
        skippedChunks++;
      }

      const pct = 20 + Math.round(((i + 1) / chunks.length) * 70);
      updateDownloadProgress(pct, 'ກຳລັງສ້າງໜ້າ PDF... (' + (i + 1) + '/' + chunks.length + ')');
    }

    if (pagesAdded === 0) {
      throw new Error('ບໍ່ສາมารถสร้างหน้า PDF ได้เลยแม้แต่หน้าเดียว');
    }

    updateDownloadProgress(95, 'ກຳລັງບັນທຶກໄฟล์...');
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 100));

    const fileName = 'สรุปข้อมูล_' + new Date().getTime() + '.pdf';
    pdf.save(fileName);

    updateDownloadProgress(100, 'ดาวโหลดสำเร็จ!');
    if (skippedChunks > 0) {
      showToast('ดาวน์โหลด PDF สำเร็จ (ข้าม ' + skippedChunks + ' ชุดที่มีปัญหา)', 'success');
    } else {
      showToast('ดาวน์โหลด PDF สำเร็จ', 'success');
    }

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('PDF export error:', error);
    showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาดในการสร้าง PDF: ' + error.message, 'error');
  } finally {
    if (container && container.parentNode) container.parentNode.removeChild(container);
    if (renderDiv && renderDiv.parentNode) renderDiv.parentNode.removeChild(renderDiv);
    hideDownloadProgress();
    if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.style.opacity = '1'; btn.style.cursor = 'pointer'; }
  }
}


let gpsModalOpening = false;

function openGpsSummaryModal(type) {
  if (gpsModalOpening) return;
  gpsModalOpening = true;

  const btn = document.getElementById('btnOpenGpsSummary');
  if (btn) {
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.style.opacity = '0.6';
    btn.style.cursor = 'not-allowed';
  }

  showLoading(0, 'ກຳລັງໂຫລດແຜນທີ່ GPS...');

  summaryDataType = type;
  document.getElementById('gpsSummaryModal').classList.add('active');

  setTimeout(() => {
    initGpsSummaryMap();
    hideLoading();

    if (btn) {
      btn.disabled = false;
      btn.style.opacity = '1';
      btn.style.cursor = 'pointer';
    }
    gpsModalOpening = false;
  }, 300);
}

function closeGpsSummaryModal() {
  document.getElementById('gpsSummaryModal').classList.remove('active');
  stopMyLocationTracking();
  if (gpsSummaryMap) {
    gpsSummaryMap.remove();
    gpsSummaryMap = null;
  }
  gpsMarkersData = {};
  currentEditingMarker = null;
  gpsCurrentSearchTerm = '';
  gpsSelectedUnits = [];
  const gpsSearchEl = document.getElementById('gpsSearchInput');
  if (gpsSearchEl) gpsSearchEl.value = '';
  const gpsUnitDropdownEl = document.getElementById('gpsUnitDropdown');
  if (gpsUnitDropdownEl) gpsUnitDropdownEl.style.display = 'none';
}

function initGpsSummaryMap() {
  if (gpsSummaryMap) {
    gpsSummaryMap.remove();
    gpsSummaryMap = null;
  }
  gpsMarkersData = {};
  populateGpsUnitFilter();

  gpsSummaryMap = L.map('gpsSummaryMap', {
    center: [17.9757, 102.6331],
    zoom: 13
  });

  L.tileLayer('https://server.arcgisonline.com/ArcGIS/rest/services/World_Imagery/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}', {
    attribution: '© Esri',
    maxZoom: 19
  }).addTo(gpsSummaryMap);

  const bounds = [];

  if (summaryDataType === 'household') {
    householdsData.forEach(household => {
      if (!household.housePosition || !household.housePosition.includes(',')) return;

      const parts = household.housePosition.split(',');
      const lat = parseFloat(parts[0].trim());
      const lng = parseFloat(parts[1].trim());
      if (isNaN(lat) || isNaN(lng)) return;

     bounds.push([lat, lng]);
      const marker = L.marker([lat, lng]).addTo(gpsSummaryMap);
     const householdMatchItems = [{ label: household.name || '-', phone: household.phone || '' }];
      (household.members || []).forEach(function(mm) {
        householdMatchItems.push({ label: mm.name || '-', phone: mm.phone || '' });
      });
     gpsMarkersData[household.householdCode] = { marker: marker, type: 'household', code: household.householdCode, matchItems: householdMatchItems, unit: household.unit || '' };

      let popupHTML = '<div style="max-width:320px; max-height:300px; overflow-y:auto;">';
      popupHTML += '<h4 style="margin-bottom:5px; color:#2e7d32;">🏠 ' + (household.name || '-') + '</h4>';
      popupHTML += '<table style="width:100%; font-size:12px; border-collapse:collapse; margin-bottom:8px;">';
      popupHTML += '<tr><td><strong>ລະຫັດ:</strong></td><td>' + household.householdCode + '</td></tr>';
      popupHTML += '<tr><td><strong>ເບີໂທລະສັບ:</strong></td><td>' + (household.phone || '-') + '</td></tr>';
      popupHTML += '<tr><td><strong>ໜ່ວຍ:</strong></td><td>' + (household.unit || '-') + '</td></tr>';
      popupHTML += '<tr><td><strong>ບ້ານ:</strong></td><td>' + (household.village || '-') + '</td></tr>';
      popupHTML += '</table>';

      if (household.members && household.members.length > 0) {
        popupHTML += '<div style="font-weight:bold; color:#667eea; margin-bottom:4px;">👥 ສະມາຊິກ (' + household.members.length + ' ຄົນ)</div>';
        popupHTML += '<table style="width:100%; font-size:11px; border-collapse:collapse;">';
        popupHTML += '<tr style="background:#667eea; color:white;"><th style="padding:2px;">ຊື່</th><th style="padding:2px;">ສາຍພົວພັນຄອບຄົວ</th><th style="padding:2px;">ເພດ</th></tr>';
        sortMembersByRelationship(household.members).forEach(m => {
          popupHTML += '<tr><td style="padding:2px; border-bottom:1px solid #eee;">' + m.name + '</td><td style="padding:2px; border-bottom:1px solid #eee;">' + (m.relationship || '-') + '</td><td style="padding:2px; border-bottom:1px solid #eee;">' + (m.gender || '-') + '</td></tr>';
        });
        popupHTML += '</table>';
      }
      popupHTML += '<div style="margin-top:10px; text-align:center;">';
      popupHTML += '<button onclick="enableMarkerEdit(\'' + household.householdCode + '\', \'household\')" style="padding:8px 16px; background:linear-gradient(135deg,#ff9800 0%,#f57c00 100%); color:white; border:none; border-radius:8px; cursor:pointer; font-weight:600; font-size:13px;">✏️ ແກ້ໄຂຕໍາແໜ່ງ</button>';
      popupHTML += '</div>';
      popupHTML += '</div>';

      marker.bindPopup(popupHTML);
    });
  } else {
    mobilesData.forEach(mobile => {
      if (!mobile.position || !mobile.position.includes(',')) return;

      const parts = mobile.position.split(',');
      const lat = parseFloat(parts[0].trim());
      const lng = parseFloat(parts[1].trim());
      if (isNaN(lat) || isNaN(lng)) return;

bounds.push([lat, lng]);
      const marker = L.marker([lat, lng]).addTo(gpsSummaryMap);
    gpsMarkersData[mobile.mobileCode] = { marker: marker, type: 'mobile', code: mobile.mobileCode, matchItems: [{ label: mobile.name || '-', phone: mobile.phone || '' }], unit: mobile.unit || '' };

      let popupHTML = '<div style="max-width:280px;">';
      popupHTML += '<h4 style="margin-bottom:5px; color:#f57c00;">🚶 ' + (mobile.name || '-') + '</h4>';
      popupHTML += '<table style="width:100%; font-size:12px;">';
      popupHTML += '<tr><td><strong>ห้องเบอร์:</strong></td><td>' + (mobile.roomNumber || '-') + '</td></tr>';
      popupHTML += '<tr><td><strong>เพศ:</strong></td><td>' + (mobile.gender || '-') + '</td></tr>';
      popupHTML += '<tr><td><strong>อาชีพ:</strong></td><td>' + (mobile.occupation || '-') + '</td></tr>';
      popupHTML += '<tr><td><strong>เจ้าของห้อง:</strong></td><td>' + (mobile.ownerName || '-') + '</td></tr>';
      popupHTML += '</table>';
      popupHTML += '<div style="margin-top:10px; text-align:center;">';
      popupHTML += '<button onclick="enableMarkerEdit(\'' + mobile.mobileCode + '\', \'mobile\')" style="padding:8px 16px; background:linear-gradient(135deg,#ff9800 0%,#f57c00 100%); color:white; border:none; border-radius:8px; cursor:pointer; font-weight:600; font-size:13px;">✏️ ແກ້ໄຂຕໍາແໜ່ງ</button>';
      popupHTML += '</div></div>';

      marker.bindPopup(popupHTML);
    });
  }

if (bounds.length > 0) {
    gpsSummaryMap.fitBounds(bounds, { padding: [50, 50] });
  }
setTimeout(() => gpsSummaryMap.invalidateSize(), 200);

  startMyLocationTracking();
}

// ===================================
// GPS MARKER EDIT MODE (ลาก Marker แก้ไขตำแหน่ง)
// ===================================
function enableMarkerEdit(code, type) {
  const entry = gpsMarkersData[code];
  if (!entry) {
    showToast('ບໍ່ພົບ Marker', 'error');
    return;
  }

  // ปิดโหมดลากของ marker ก่อนหน้า (ถ้ามี) เพื่อให้ลากได้ทีละจุด
  if (currentEditingMarker && currentEditingMarker.marker) {
    currentEditingMarker.marker.dragging.disable();
  }

  const marker = entry.marker;
  marker.dragging.enable();
  currentEditingMarker = entry;

  marker.closePopup();
  showToast('🖐️ ສາມາດລາກ Marker ໄດ້ແລ້ວ! ລາກໄປບ່ອນທີ່ຖືກຕ້ອງ ແລ້ວປ່ອຍມືເພື່ອບັນທຶກອັດຕະໂນມັດ', 'success');

  marker.off('dragend');
  marker.on('dragend', function() {
    const pos = marker.getLatLng();
    const newPositionStr = pos.lat.toFixed(6) + ', ' + pos.lng.toFixed(6);
    saveMarkerNewPosition(code, type, newPositionStr, marker);
  });
}

function saveMarkerNewPosition(code, type, newPositionStr, marker) {
  showLoading(0, 'ກຳລັງບັນທຶກຕໍາແໜ່ງໃໝ່...');

  const successHandler = function(result) {
    hideLoading();
    if (result.success) {
      showToast('✅ ບັນທຶກຕໍາແໜ່ງໃໝ່ສຳເລັດ', 'success');

      if (type === 'household') {
        const h = householdsData.find(function(x) { return x.householdCode === code; });
        if (h) {
          h.housePosition = newPositionStr;
          saveToIndexedDB('households', h);
        }
      } else {
        const m = mobilesData.find(function(x) { return x.mobileCode === code; });
        if (m) {
          m.position = newPositionStr;
          saveToIndexedDB('mobiles', m);
        }
      }

      marker.dragging.disable();
      currentEditingMarker = null;
    } else {
      showToast('❌ ' + result.message, 'error');
    }
  };

  const failureHandler = function(error) {
    hideLoading();
    showToast('❌ ເກີດຂໍ້ຜິດພາດ: ' + error.message, 'error');
  };

if (type === 'household') {
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(successHandler)
      .withFailureHandler(failureHandler)
      .updateHouseholdPosition(code, newPositionStr, villageSheetId);
  } else {
    google.script.run
      .withSuccessHandler(successHandler)
      .withFailureHandler(failureHandler)
      .updateMobilePosition(code, newPositionStr, villageSheetId);
  }
}

// ===================================
// GPS REAL-TIME SEARCH (ค้นหาชื่อ/เบอร์โทร แบบเรียลไทม์)
// ===================================
function filterGpsMarkers(searchTerm) {
  gpsCurrentSearchTerm = (searchTerm || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  applyGpsFilters();
}

function applyGpsFilters() {
  if (!gpsSummaryMap) return;
  const term = gpsCurrentSearchTerm;
  const matchedBounds = [];

  Object.keys(gpsMarkersData).forEach(function(code) {
    const entry = gpsMarkersData[code];
    entry.marker.unbindTooltip();

    const unitMatch = gpsSelectedUnits.length === 0 || gpsSelectedUnits.indexOf(entry.unit) !== -1;

    let matchedItem = null;
    if (term) {
      (entry.matchItems || []).forEach(function(item) {
        if (matchedItem) return;
        const nameMatch = item.label && item.label.toLowerCase().indexOf(term) !== -1;
        const phoneMatch = item.phone && item.phone.indexOf(term) !== -1;
        if (nameMatch || phoneMatch) matchedItem = item;
      });
    }
    const searchMatch = !term || !!matchedItem;
    const visible = unitMatch && searchMatch;

    if (visible) {
      if (!gpsSummaryMap.hasLayer(entry.marker)) {
        entry.marker.addTo(gpsSummaryMap);
      }
      if (term && matchedItem) {
        entry.marker.bindTooltip(matchedItem.label, {
          permanent: true,
          direction: 'top',
          className: 'gps-search-label'
        }).openTooltip();
      }
      matchedBounds.push(entry.marker.getLatLng());
    } else {
      if (gpsSummaryMap.hasLayer(entry.marker)) {
        gpsSummaryMap.removeLayer(entry.marker);
      }
    }
  });

  if ((term || gpsSelectedUnits.length > 0) && matchedBounds.length > 0) {
    if (matchedBounds.length === 1) {
      gpsSummaryMap.setView(matchedBounds[0], 17);
    } else {
      gpsSummaryMap.fitBounds(matchedBounds, { padding: [60, 60] });
    }
  }
}

function clearGpsSearch() {
  const gpsSearchEl = document.getElementById('gpsSearchInput');
  if (gpsSearchEl) gpsSearchEl.value = '';
  gpsCurrentSearchTerm = '';
  applyGpsFilters();
}

// ===================================
// GPS UNIT FILTER (ตัวกรองหน่วย เลือกได้หลายรายการ)
// ===================================
function populateGpsUnitFilter() {
  const dropdown = document.getElementById('gpsUnitDropdown');
  if (!dropdown) return;

  gpsSelectedUnits = [];
  updateGpsUnitCountBadge();
  dropdown.innerHTML = '';

  const unitSet = new Set();
  if (summaryDataType === 'household') {
    householdsData.forEach(function(h) { if (h.unit) unitSet.add(h.unit); });
  } else {
    mobilesData.forEach(function(m) { if (m.unit) unitSet.add(m.unit); });
  }

  const sortedUnits = Array.from(unitSet).sort();

  if (sortedUnits.length === 0) {
    dropdown.innerHTML = '<div style="color:#999; padding:5px; text-align:center;">ບໍ່ມີຂໍ້ມູນໜ່ວຍ</div>';
    return;
  }

  sortedUnits.forEach(function(unit) {
    const label = document.createElement('label');
    label.style.cssText = 'display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; padding:8px 6px; cursor:pointer; border-bottom:1px solid #f0f0f0;';
    label.innerHTML = '<input type="checkbox" value="' + unit + '" onchange="toggleGpsUnit(\'' + unit + '\', this.checked)" style="width:18px; height:18px; cursor:pointer;"> <span>' + unit + '</span>';
    dropdown.appendChild(label);
  });
}

function toggleGpsUnitDropdown() {
  const dropdown = document.getElementById('gpsUnitDropdown');
  if (!dropdown) return;
  dropdown.style.display = (dropdown.style.display === 'none' || !dropdown.style.display) ? 'block' : 'none';
}

function toggleGpsUnit(unit, checked) {
  if (checked) {
    if (gpsSelectedUnits.indexOf(unit) === -1) gpsSelectedUnits.push(unit);
  } else {
    gpsSelectedUnits = gpsSelectedUnits.filter(function(u) { return u !== unit; });
  }
  updateGpsUnitCountBadge();
  applyGpsFilters();
}

function updateGpsUnitCountBadge() {
  const badge = document.getElementById('gpsUnitCount');
  if (badge) badge.textContent = gpsSelectedUnits.length;
}

// ===================================
// MY LIVE LOCATION ON GPS SUMMARY MAP
// ===================================
function startMyLocationTracking() {
  if (!navigator.geolocation) {
    showToast('ບາວເຊີບໍ່ຮອງຮັບ GPS', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if (myLocationWatchId !== null) return;

  myLocationWatchId = navigator.geolocation.watchPosition(
    function(position) {
      const lat = position.coords.latitude;
      const lng = position.coords.longitude;

  if (!myLocationMarker) {
        const myIcon = L.divIcon({
          html: '<div style="font-size: 36px; line-height: 36px; text-align:center; filter: drop-shadow(0 3px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.5));">📍</div>',
          className: '',
          iconSize: [36, 36],
          iconAnchor: [18, 36]
        });
        myLocationMarker = L.marker([lat, lng], { icon: myIcon, zIndexOffset: 1000 }).addTo(gpsSummaryMap);
        myLocationMarker.bindTooltip('📍 ຕໍາແໜ່ງຂອງຂ້ອຍ', { permanent: false });
      } else {
        myLocationMarker.setLatLng([lat, lng]);
      }
    },
    function(error) {
      console.error('Geolocation error:', error);
      showToast('ບໍ່ສາມາດເຂົ້າເຖິງຕໍາແໜ່ງ GPS ໄດ້: ' + error.message, 'error');
    },
    { enableHighAccuracy: true, maximumAge: 5000, timeout: 15000 }
  );

  const btn = document.getElementById('btnGpsMyLocationToggle');
  if (btn) btn.textContent = '🛑 ຢຸດຕິດຕາມຕໍາແໜ່ງ';
}

function stopMyLocationTracking() {
  if (myLocationWatchId !== null) {
    navigator.geolocation.clearWatch(myLocationWatchId);
    myLocationWatchId = null;
  }
  if (myLocationMarker && gpsSummaryMap) {
    gpsSummaryMap.removeLayer(myLocationMarker);
  }
  myLocationMarker = null;

  const btn = document.getElementById('btnGpsMyLocationToggle');
  if (btn) btn.textContent = '📍 ຕໍາແໜ່ງຂອງຂ້ອຍ';
}

function toggleMyLocationTracking() {
  if (myLocationWatchId !== null) {
    stopMyLocationTracking();
  } else {
    startMyLocationTracking();
  }
}

window.addEventListener('load', function() {
  const gpsSearchEl = document.getElementById('gpsSearchInput');
  if (gpsSearchEl) {
    gpsSearchEl.addEventListener('input', function() {
      filterGpsMarkers(this.value);
    });
  }
});

console.log('✅ Summary report system loaded');
// ===================================
// REAL-TIME SEARCH (ค้นหาทันทีจากข้อมูลในเครื่อง ไม่ต้องรอโหลดจากเซิร์ฟเวอร์)
// ===================================
// ===================================
// PWA: Service Worker (best-effort เท่านั้น)
// ===================================
// ⚠️ หมายเหตุ: Google Apps Script รันในกรอบ (iframe) แบบพิเศษของ Google 
// ทำให้ Service Worker อาจลงทะเบียนไม่สำเร็จในบางกรณี — ถ้าล้มเหลว ระบบจะไม่พัง 
// (ข้อมูล offline หลักยังทำงานผ่าน IndexedDB ที่มีอยู่แล้วตามปกติ)
if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
  try {
    const swCode = `
      const CACHE_NAME = 'population-app-v1';
      self.addEventListener('install', function(e) { self.skipWaiting(); });
      self.addEventListener('activate', function(e) { self.clients.claim(); });
      self.addEventListener('fetch', function(e) {
        // ปล่อยผ่านตามปกติ ไม่แคชอะไรเพิ่ม (ข้อมูลหลักใช้ IndexedDB อยู่แล้ว)
      });
    `;
    const swBlob = new Blob([swCode], { type: 'application/javascript' });
    const swUrl = URL.createObjectURL(swBlob);

    navigator.serviceWorker.register(swUrl)
      .then(function() {
        console.log('✅ Service Worker ລົງທະບຽນສຳເລັດ');
      })
      .catch(function(err) {
        console.log('⚠️ Service Worker ລົງທະບຽນບໍ່ສຳເລັດ (ບໍ່ເປັນຫຍັງ, ໃຊ້ IndexedDB ແທນ):', err.message);
      });
  } catch (e) {
    console.log('⚠️ Service Worker ບໍ່ຮອງຮັບໃນສະພາບແວດລ້ອมนี้:', e.message);
  }
}

    });
  }
});
